\displaystyle\leq 4\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left(\beta C_{0}^{2(N-1)}M^{2}_{\text{prod}}M_{\text{sum}}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\sum_{m=1}^{M^{(n)}}\sigma^{(n)2}_{m,t}({\bm{z}}^{(n-1)}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n-1)}_{t+1}),{\bm{x}}^{(n)}_{t+1})\right)

(C.77)

\displaystyle\leq 4\beta C_{0}^{2(N-1)}M^{2}_{\text{prod}}M_{\text{sum}}sum_{n=1}^{N}\sum_{m=1}^{M^{(n)}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\Bigl[\sigma^{(n)2}_{m,t}({\bm{z}}^{(n-1)}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n-1)}_{t+1}),{\bm{x}}^{(n)}_{t+1})\Bigr].

(C.78)

Furthermore, by using the same argument as in Lemma 5.3 and 5.4 of
Srinivas et al., 2010
, under the assumption
k^{(n)}(\cdot,\cdot)\leq 1
we get

\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sigma^{(n)2}_{m,t}({\bm{z}}^{(n-1)}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n-1)}_{t+1}),{\bm{x}}^{(n)}_{t+1})\leq\frac{2}{\log(1+\sigma^{-2})}\gamma^{(n)}_{m,T}\leq\frac{2}{\log(1+\sigma^{-2})}\gamma_{T}.

(C.79)

Thus, from
eqs.

C.78
and
C.79
we obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}r^{2}_{t}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{8\beta C_{0}^{2(N-1)}M^{2}_{\text{prod}}M^{2}_{\text{sum}}}{\log(1+\sigma^{-2})}\gamma_{T}.

(C.80)

Hence, by using
eqs.

C.76
and
C.80
, it follows that

R_{t}\leq\sqrt{\frac{8\beta C_{0}^{2(N-1)}M^{2}_{\text{prod}}M^{2}_{\text{sum}}}{\log(1+\sigma^{-2})}T\gamma_{T}}.

(C.81)

Finally, since
r^{(\text{S})}_{T}
satisfies

Tr^{(\text{S})}_{T}\leq\sum_{t=1}^{T}r_{t}=R_{T}\leq\sqrt{\frac{8\beta C_{0}^{2(N-1)}M^{2}_{\text{prod}}M^{2}_{\text{sum}}}{\log(1+\sigma^{-2})}T\gamma_{T}},

(C.82)

the following inequality holds:

r^{(\text{S})}_{T}\leq T^{-1/2}\sqrt{\frac{8\beta C_{0}^{2(N-1)}M^{2}_{\text{prod}}M^{2}_{\text{sum}}}{\log(1+\sigma^{-2})}\gamma_{T}}.

(C.83)

∎

C.3
Optimistic Improvement-based AF

In this subsection, we consider sequential observations of a cascade process from stage
1
to
N
.
For each iteration
t\in\{0,N,2N,\ldots\}\equiv N\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
, users determine
{\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1}
and observe
\bm{y}_{t+1}^{(1)}={\bm{f}}^{(1)}({\bm{0}},{\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1})
.
After that, users choose
{\bm{x}}^{(2)}_{t+2}
and observe
\bm{y}_{t+2}^{(2)}={\bm{f}}^{(2)}(\bm{y}^{(1)}_{t+1},{\bm{x}}^{(2)}_{t+2})
.
By repeating this operation, users obtain
y^{(N)}_{t+N}={\bm{f}}^{(N)}(\bm{y}^{(N-1)}_{t+N-1},{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{t+N})
finally.
We design the CI-based AF according to the following strategy:
(1) given an observation
{\bm{y}}^{(n)}
, we seek the maximum of
F
if it is expected to be found;
(2) if the maximum is not expected to be found, we collect the information by using another policy.
We use the optimistic improvement for (1), and we adopt uncertainty sampling (US) policy for (2).
First, we define the pessimistic maximum of
F(\bm{x}^{(1)},\dots,\bm{x}^{(N)})
as

Q_{T}=\max_{({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)})}\left(\tilde{\mu}^{(N)}_{1,T}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)})-\beta^{1/2}\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)}_{1,T}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)})\right).

(C.84)

In addition, given the observation
{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)}
in stage
n-1
, we define the pessimistic maximum of
F
obtained through
{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)}
as follows:

\mathrm{LCB}^{(F)}_{t}({\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})=\max_{{\bm{x}}^{(n:N)}}\left(\tilde{\mu}^{(N)}_{1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(n:N)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})-\beta^{1/2}\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)}_{1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(n:N)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})\right),

(C.85)

where the
\max
operator is not necessary when
n=N
.
Similarly, the optimistic maximum for given the input
(\bm{y}^{(n-1)},\bm{x}^{(n)})
is defined as follows:

\mathrm{UCB}^{(F)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})=\max_{{\bm{x}}^{(n+1:N)}}\left(\tilde{\mu}^{(N)}_{1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(n:N)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})+\beta^{1/2}\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)}_{1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(n:N)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})\right).

(C.86)

Then, we define the optimistic improvement w.r.t.
(\bm{y}^{(n-1)},{\bm{x}}^{(n)})
as follows:

a^{(n)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})=\mathrm{UCB}^{(F)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})-\max\{\mathrm{LCB}^{(F)}_{t}({\bm{y}}^{(n-1)}),Q_{t+n-1}\}.

(C.87)

Furthermore, we define the maximum uncertainty

b^{(n)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})=\max_{({\bm{x}}^{(n+1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)})}\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)}_{1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)}).

(C.88)

Using
eqs.

C.87
and
C.88
, optimistic improvement-based AF (presented as CI-based AF in
section

3.2
)
c^{(n)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})
is defined as

c^{(n)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})=\max\left\{a^{(n)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)}),\eta_{t}b^{(n)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})\right\},

(C.89)

where
\eta_{t}
is some learning rate tends to zero.
Therefore, given the observation
\bm{y}^{(n-1)}
at iteration
t
, the next observation point is given by

{\bm{x}}^{(n)}_{t+n}=\argmax_{{\bm{x}}^{(n)}\in\mathcal{X}^{(n)}}c^{(n)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}}_{t+n-1}^{(n-1)}),

(C.90)

where
{\bm{y}}^{(0)}={\bm{0}}
.

Theorem C.10
.

Assume that
C.1
,
C.3
and
C.4
hold.
Also
assume that
\tilde{\bm{\mu}}^{(n)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(s+1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}})\in\mathcal{Y}^{(n)}

for any
s\in\{0,\ldots,N-1\}
,

n\in\{s+1,\ldots,N\}
,
{\bm{y}}\in\mathcal{Y}^{(s)}
,
t\geq 1
and
({\bm{x}}^{(s+1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n)})\in\mathcal{X}^{(s+1)}\times\cdots\times\mathcal{X}^{(n)}
.
Let
\xi
be a positive number, and define
\beta=B^{2}
and
\eta_{t}=(1+\log t)^{-1}
.
Then, when the optimization is performed using
eq.

C.90
, the estimated solution
(\hat{\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{T},\ldots,\hat{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{T})
satisfies that

F({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{\ast},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{\ast})-F(\hat{\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{T},\ldots,\hat{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{T})<\xi,

(C.91)

where
T
is the smallest positive integer satisfying
T\in N\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
and

\frac{8\beta C^{2}_{4}M^{2}_{\text{sum}}N}{\log(1+\sigma^{-2})}\gamma_{T}\eta^{-2N-2}_{T}T^{-1}<\xi^{2}.

(C.92)

Here,
C_{4}
is the positive constant given by

C_{1}=\max\{1,L_{f},L^{-1}_{f}\},C_{2}=4NM^{2}_{\text{prod}}M_{\text{sum}}C^{2N-3}_{0}C^{N}_{1},C_{3}=NC_{2}^{N},C_{4}=(2\beta^{1/2}+2)^{N}C^{N}_{3}.

(C.93)

In order to prove
theorem

C.10
, we give four lemmas.

Lemma C.11
.

Assume that the same condition as in
theorem

C.10
holds.
Let
s\in\{1,\ldots,N-1\}
and
n\in\{s+1,\ldots,N\}
.
Then, for any iteration
t\geq 1
, element
m\in[M^{(n)}]
and input
{\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}
, the following inequality holds:

\displaystyle|\sigma^{(n)}_{m,t}(\tilde{\bm{\mu}}^{(n-1)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(s)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n-1)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s-1)}),{\bm{x}}^{(n)})-\sigma^{(n)}_{m,t}(\tilde{\bm{\mu}}^{(n-1)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(s+1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n-1)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s)}),{\bm{x}}^{(n)})|

(C.94)

\displaystyle\leq 2M_{\text{prod}}C_{0}^{N-1}\sum_{p=0}^{n-s-1}\sum_{i=1}^{M^{(n-1-p)}}\Bigl[\sigma^{(n-1-p)}_{i,t}(\tilde{\bm{\mu}}^{(n-2-p)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(s:n-2-p)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s-1)}),{\bm{x}}^{(n-1-p)})\Bigr].

(C.95)

Proof.

From Lipschitz continuity of
\sigma^{(n)}_{m,t}(\cdot)
, the following holds:

\displaystyle|\sigma^{(n)}_{m,t}(\tilde{\bm{\mu}}^{(n-1)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(s)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n-1)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s-1)}),{\bm{x}}^{(n)})-\sigma^{(n)}_{m,t}(\tilde{\bm{\mu}}^{(n-1)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(s+1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n-1)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s)}),{\bm{x}}^{(n)})|

(C.96)

\displaystyle\leq L_{\sigma}\|\tilde{\bm{\mu}}^{(n-1)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(s)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n-1)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s-1)})-\tilde{\bm{\mu}}^{(n-1)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(s+1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n-1)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s)})\|_{1}

(C.97)

\displaystyle=L_{\sigma}\sum_{j=1}^{M^{(n-1)}}|\tilde{\mu}^{(n-1)}_{j,t}({\bm{x}}^{(s)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n-1)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s-1)})-\tilde{\mu}^{(n-1)}_{j,t}({\bm{x}}^{(s+1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n-1)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s)})|

(C.98)

\displaystyle=L_{\sigma}\sum_{j=1}^{M^{(n-1)}}|{\mu}^{(n-1)}_{j,t}(\tilde{\bm{\mu}}^{(n-2)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(s:n-2)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s-1)}),{\bm{x}}^{(n-1)})-{\mu}^{(n-1)}_{j,t}(\tilde{\bm{\mu}}^{(n-2)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(s+1:n-2)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s)}),{\bm{x}}^{(n-1)})|.

(C.99)

Here, noting that

\displaystyle f^{(k)}_{m}({\bm{y}},{\bm{x}})-\beta^{1/2}\sigma^{(k)}_{m,t}({\bm{y}},{\bm{x}})

\displaystyle\leq\mu^{(k)}_{m,t}({\bm{y}},{\bm{x}})

(C.100)

\displaystyle\leq f^{(k)}_{m}({\bm{y}},{\bm{x}})+\beta^{1/2}\sigma^{(k)}_{m,t}({\bm{y}},{\bm{x}}),

(C.101)

we have

\displaystyle|

\displaystyle\mu^{(k)}_{m,t}({\bm{y}},{\bm{x}})-\mu^{(k)}_{m,t}({\bm{y}}^{\prime},{\bm{x}})|

(C.102)

\displaystyle\leq|f^{(k)}_{m}({\bm{y}},{\bm{x}})-f^{(k)}_{m}({\bm{y}}^{\prime},{\bm{x}})|+\beta^{1/2}\sigma^{(k)}_{m,t}({\bm{y}},{\bm{x}})+\beta^{1/2}\sigma^{(k)}_{m,t}({\bm{y}}^{\prime},{\bm{x}})

(C.103)

\displaystyle\leq|f^{(k)}_{m}({\bm{y}},{\bm{x}})-f^{(k)}_{m}({\bm{y}}^{\prime},{\bm{x}})|+\beta^{1/2}|\sigma^{(k)}_{m,t}({\bm{y}},{\bm{x}})-\sigma^{(k)}_{m,t}({\bm{y}}^{\prime},{\bm{x}})|+2\beta^{1/2}\sigma^{(k)}_{m,t}({\bm{y}}^{\prime},{\bm{x}})

(C.104)

\displaystyle\leq(L_{f}+\beta^{1/2}_{t}L_{\sigma})\|{\bm{y}}-{\bm{y}}^{\prime}\|_{1}+2\beta^{1/2}\sigma^{(k)}_{m,t}({\bm{y}}^{\prime},{\bm{x}}).

(C.105)

Therefore, by substituting
eq.

C.105
into
eq.

C.99
, it follows that

\displaystyle|\sigma^{(n)}_{m,t}(\tilde{\bm{\mu}}^{(n-1)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(s)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n-1)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s-1)}),{\bm{x}}^{(n)})-\sigma^{(n)}_{m,t}(\tilde{\bm{\mu}}^{(n-1)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(s+1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n-1)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s)}),{\bm{x}}^{(n)})|

(C.106)

\displaystyle\leq 2\beta^{1/2}L_{\sigma}\sum_{j=1}^{M^{(n-1)}}\sigma^{(n-1)}_{j,t}\bigl(\tilde{\bm{\mu}}^{(n-2)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(s+1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n-2)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s)}),{\bm{x}}^{(n-1)})+L_{\sigma}M^{(n-1)}(L_{f}+\beta^{1/2}L_{\sigma})

(C.107)

\displaystyle\quad\cdot\|\tilde{\bm{\mu}}^{(n-2)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(s)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n-2)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s-1)})-\tilde{\bm{\mu}}^{(n-2)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(s+1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n-2)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s)})\|_{1}

(C.108)

\displaystyle\leq 2\beta^{1/2}L_{\sigma}\sum_{j=1}^{M^{(n-1)}}\sigma^{(n-1)}_{j,t}\bigl(\tilde{\bm{\mu}}^{(n-2)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(s+1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n-2)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s)}),{\bm{x}}^{(n-1)})

(C.109)

\displaystyle\quad+L_{\sigma}M^{(n-1)}(L_{f}+\beta^{1/2}L_{\sigma})\sum_{i=1}^{M^{(n-2)}}|\tilde{\mu}^{(n-2)}_{i,t}({\bm{x}}^{(s:n-2)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s-1)})-\tilde{\mu}^{(n-2)}_{i,t}({\bm{x}}^{(s+1:n-2)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s)})|

(C.110)

\displaystyle\leq 2\beta^{1/2}L_{\sigma}\sum_{j=1}^{M^{(n-1)}}\sigma^{(n-1)}_{j,t}(\tilde{\bm{\mu}}^{(n-2)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(s:n-2)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s-1)}),{\bm{x}}^{(n-1)})

(C.111)

\displaystyle\quad+2\beta^{1/2}L_{\sigma}M^{(n-1)}(L_{f}+\beta^{1/2}L_{\sigma})\cdot\sum_{i=1}^{M^{(n-2)}}\sigma^{(n-2)}_{i,t}(\tilde{\bm{\mu}}^{(n-3)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(s)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n-3)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s-1)}),{\bm{x}}^{(n-2)})

(C.112)

\displaystyle+L_{\sigma}M^{(n-1)}M^{(n-2)}(L_{f}+\beta^{1/2}L_{\sigma})^{2}\sum_{q=1}^{M^{(n-3)}}\Bigl[|\tilde{\mu}^{(n-3)}_{q,t}({\bm{x}}^{(s:n-3)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s-1)})-\tilde{\mu}^{(n-3)}_{q,t}({\bm{x}}^{(s+1:n-3)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s)})|\Bigr]

(C.113)

\displaystyle\leq

(C.114)

(C.115)

\displaystyle\leq 2\beta^{1/2}L_{\sigma}M_{\text{prod}}(L_{f}+\beta^{1/2}L_{\sigma}+1)^{N-2}

(C.116)

\displaystyle\quad\cdot\sum_{p=0}^{n-s-2}\sum_{i=1}^{M^{(n-1-p)}}\Bigl[\sigma^{(n-1-p)}_{i,t}\Bigl(\tilde{\bm{\mu}}^{(n-2-p)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(s:n-2-p)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s-1)}),{\bm{x}}^{(n-1-p)})\Bigr]

(C.117)

\displaystyle\quad+2M_{\text{prod}}\beta^{1/2}L_{\sigma}(L_{f}+\beta^{1/2}L_{\sigma}+1)^{N-2}\sum_{q=1}^{M^{(s)}}\sigma^{(s)}_{q,t}({\bm{z}}^{(s-1)},{\bm{x}}^{(s)})

(C.118)

\displaystyle\leq 2M_{\text{prod}}(L_{f}+\beta^{1/2}L_{\sigma}+1)^{N-1}\sum_{p=0}^{n-s-1}\sum_{i=1}^{M^{(n-1-p)}}\Bigl[\sigma^{(n-1-p)}_{i,t}(\tilde{\bm{\mu}}^{(n-2-p)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(s:n-2-p)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s-1)}),{\bm{x}}^{(n-1-p)})\Bigr]

(C.119)

\displaystyle\leq 2M_{\text{prod}}C^{N-1}_{0}\sum_{p=0}^{n-s-1}\sum_{i=1}^{M^{(n-1-p)}}\Bigl[\sigma^{(n-1-p)}_{i,t}\bigl(\tilde{\bm{\mu}}^{(n-2-p)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(s)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n-2-p)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s-1)}),{\bm{x}}^{(n-1-p)}\bigr)\Bigr].

(C.120)

∎

Lemma C.12
.

Assume that the same condition as in
theorem

C.10
holds.
Let
s\in\{1,\ldots,N-1\}
, and let
j\geq 0
be an integer with
s+j\leq N
.
Then, for any iteration
t\geq 1
, element
m\in[M^{(n)}]
and input
{\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}
, the following inequality holds:

\displaystyle\tilde{\sigma}^{(N-j)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(s)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N-j)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s-1)})

\displaystyle\leq\tilde{C}_{2}\tilde{\sigma}^{(N-j)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(s+1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N-j)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s)})+\tilde{C}_{2}\sum_{i=1}^{M^{(s)}}\sigma^{(s)}_{i,t}({\bm{z}}^{(s-1)},{\bm{x}}^{(s)})

(C.121)

\displaystyle\quad+\tilde{C}_{2}\tilde{\sigma}^{(N-j-1)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(s)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N-j-1)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s-1)}),

(C.122)

where

\displaystyle\tilde{\sigma}^{(N-j)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(s)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N-j)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s-1)})

(C.123)

\displaystyle=\sum_{p=j}^{N-s}\prod_{q=1}^{p}M^{(N-q+1)}L_{f}^{p}\sum_{i=1}^{M^{(N-p)}}\Bigl[\sigma^{(N-p)}_{i,t}(\tilde{\bm{\mu}}^{(N-p-1)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(s)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N-p-1)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s-1)}),{\bm{x}}^{(N-p)})\Bigr]

(C.124)

and
\tilde{C}_{2}=4NM^{2}_{\text{prod}}M_{\text{sum}}C^{2N-2}_{0}C^{N}_{1}
.

Proof.

From the definition of
\tilde{\sigma}^{(N-j)}_{t}

({\bm{x}}^{(s)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N-j)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s-1)})
, the following inequality holds:

\displaystyle\tilde{\sigma}^{(N-j)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(s)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N-j)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s-1)})

(C.125)

\displaystyle=\sum_{p=j}^{N-s}\prod_{q=1}^{p}M^{(N-q+1)}L_{f}^{p}\sum_{i=1}^{M^{(N-p)}}\Bigl[\sigma^{(N-p)}_{i,t}(\tilde{\bm{\mu}}^{(N-p-1)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(s)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N-p-1)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s-1)}),{\bm{x}}^{(N-p)})\Bigr]

(C.126)

\displaystyle\leq M_{\text{prod}}C^{N-1}_{0}\sum_{p=j}^{N-s}\sum_{i=1}^{M^{(N-p)}}\Bigl[\sigma^{(N-p)}_{i,t}(\tilde{\bm{\mu}}^{(N-p-1)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(s)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N-p-1)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s-1)}),{\bm{x}}^{(N-p)})\Bigr]

(C.127)

\displaystyle=M_{\text{prod}}C^{N-1}_{0}\sum_{p=j}^{N-s-1}\sum_{i=1}^{M^{(N-p)}}\Bigl[\sigma^{(N-p)}_{i,t}(\tilde{\bm{\mu}}^{(N-p-1)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(s+1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N-p-1)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s)}),{\bm{x}}^{(N-p)})\Bigr]

(C.128)

\displaystyle+M_{\text{prod}}C^{N-1}_{0}\sum_{p=j}^{N-s-1}\sum_{i=1}^{M^{(N-p)}}\left(\sigma^{(N-p)}_{i,t}(\tilde{\bm{\mu}}^{(N-p-1)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(s)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N-p-1)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s-1)}),{\bm{x}}^{(N-p)})\right.

(C.129)

\displaystyle\left.\quad-\sigma^{(N-p)}_{i,t}(\tilde{\bm{\mu}}^{(N-p-1)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(s+1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N-p-1)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s)}),{\bm{x}}^{(N-p)})\right)+M_{\text{prod}}C^{N-1}_{0}\sum_{i=1}^{M^{(s)}}\sigma^{(s)}_{i,t}({\bm{z}}^{(s-1)},{\bm{x}}^{(s)}).

(C.130)

Thus, from
lemma

C.11
we get

\displaystyle|\sigma^{(N-p)}_{i,t}(\tilde{\bm{\mu}}^{(N-p-1)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(s)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N-p-1)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s-1)}),{\bm{x}}^{(N-p)})

(C.131)

\displaystyle\qquad-\sigma^{(N-p)}_{i,t}(\tilde{\bm{\mu}}^{(N-p-1)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(s+1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N-p-1)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s)}),{\bm{x}}^{(N-p)})|

(C.132)

\displaystyle\leq 2M_{\text{prod}}C^{N-1}_{0}\sum_{r=0}^{N-p-s-1}

(C.133)

\displaystyle\quad\cdot\sum_{j=1}^{M^{(N-p-1-r)}}\Bigl[\sigma^{(N-p-1-r)}_{j,t}\Bigl(\tilde{\bm{\mu}}^{(N-p-2-r)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(s)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N-p-2-r)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s-1)}),{\bm{x}}^{(N-p-1-r)}\Bigr)\Bigr].

(C.134)

By using this,
\tilde{\sigma}^{(N-j)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(s)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N-j)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s-1)})
can be written as

\displaystyle\tilde{\sigma}^{(N-j)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(s)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N-j)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s-1)})

(C.135)

\displaystyle\leq M_{\text{prod}}C^{N-2}_{0}\sum_{p=j}^{N-s-1}\sum_{i=1}^{M^{(N-p)}}\sigma^{(N-p)}_{i,t}\Bigl(\tilde{\bm{\mu}}^{(N-p-1)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(s+1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N-p-1)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s)}),{\bm{x}}^{(N-p)}\Bigr)

(C.136)

\displaystyle\quad+M_{\text{prod}}C^{N-2}_{0}\sum_{i=1}^{M^{(s)}}\sigma^{(s)}_{i,t}({\bm{z}}^{(s-1)},{\bm{x}}^{(s)})+2M^{2}_{\text{prod}}C^{2N-2}_{0}M_{\text{sum}}

(C.137)

\displaystyle\quad\cdot\sum_{p=j}^{N-s-1}\sum_{r=0}^{N-p-s-1}\sum_{j=1}^{M^{(N-p-1-r)}}\Bigl[\sigma^{(N-p-1-r)}_{j,t}\Bigl(\tilde{\bm{\mu}}^{(N-p-2-r)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(s:N-p-2-r)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s-1)}),{\bm{x}}^{(N-p-1-r)}\Bigr)\Bigr].

(C.138)

Here, we set

v=p+r
. Then, noting that
|\{(p,r)\mid p+r=a\}|\leq 2a
, we obtain

\displaystyle\tilde{\sigma}^{(N-j)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(s)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N-j)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s-1)})

(C.139)

\displaystyle\leq M_{\text{prod}}C^{N-2}_{0}\sum_{p=j}^{N-s-1}\sum_{i=1}^{M^{(N-p)}}\sigma^{(N-p)}_{i,t}\Bigl(\tilde{\bm{\mu}}^{(N-p-1)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(s+1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N-p-1)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s)}),{\bm{x}}^{(N-p)}\Bigr)

(C.140)

\displaystyle+2M^{2}_{\text{prod}}C^{2N-2}_{0}M_{\text{sum}}\sum_{v=j}^{N-s-1}2N\sum_{j=1}^{M^{(N-v-1)}}\sigma^{(N-v-1)}_{j,t}\Bigl(\tilde{\bm{\mu}}^{(N-v-2)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(s)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N-v-2)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s-1)}),{\bm{x}}^{(N-v-1)}\Bigr)

(C.141)

\displaystyle+M_{\text{prod}}C^{N-2}_{0}\sum_{i=1}^{M^{(s)}}\sigma^{(s)}_{i,t}({\bm{z}}^{(s-1)},{\bm{x}}^{(s)})

(C.142)

\displaystyle\leq 4NM^{2}_{\text{prod}}C^{2N-2}_{0}M_{\text{sum}}\sum_{p=j}^{N-s-1}\sum_{i=1}^{M^{(N-p)}}\sigma^{(N-p)}_{i,t}\Bigl(\tilde{\bm{\mu}}^{(N-p-1)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(s+1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N-p-1)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s)}),{\bm{x}}^{(N-p)}\Bigr)

(C.143)

\displaystyle+4NM^{2}_{\text{prod}}C^{2N-2}_{0}M_{\text{sum}}\sum_{v=j}^{N-s-1}\sum_{j=1}^{M^{(N-v-1)}}\sigma^{(N-v-1)}_{j,t}\Bigl(\tilde{\bm{\mu}}^{(N-v-2)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(s)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N-v-2)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s-1)}),{\bm{x}}^{(N-v-1)}\Bigr)

(C.144)

\displaystyle+4NM^{2}_{\text{prod}}C^{2N-2}_{0}M_{\text{sum}}C^{N}_{1}\sum_{i=1}^{M^{(s)}}\sigma^{(s)}_{i,t}({\bm{z}}^{(s-1)},{\bm{x}}^{(s)})

(C.145)

\displaystyle\leq\tilde{C}_{2}\tilde{\sigma}^{(N-j)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(s+1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N-j)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s)})+\tilde{C}_{2}\tilde{\sigma}^{(N-j-1)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(s)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N-j-1)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s-1)})

(C.146)

\displaystyle\quad+\tilde{C}_{2}\sum_{i=1}^{M^{(s)}}\sigma^{(s)}_{i,t}({\bm{z}}^{(s-1)},{\bm{x}}^{(s)}).

(C.147)

∎

Lemma C.13
.

Assume that the same condition as in
theorem

C.10
holds.
Let
s\in\{1,\ldots,N-1\}
and
n\in\{s+1,\ldots,N\}
.
Then, for any iteration
t\geq 1
, element
m\in[M^{(n)}]
and input
{\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}
, the following inequality holds:

\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)}_{1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(s:N)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s-1)})\leq C_{3}\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)}_{1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(s+1:N)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s)})+C_{3}\sum_{i=1}^{M^{(s)}}\sigma^{(s)}_{i,t}({\bm{z}}^{(s-1)},{\bm{x}}^{(s)}).

(C.148)

Proof.

By repeatedly using
lemma

C.12
, we obtain

\displaystyle\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)}_{1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(s)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s-1)})

(C.149)

\displaystyle=\tilde{\sigma}^{(N-0)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(s)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N-0)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s-1)})

(C.150)

\displaystyle\leq\tilde{C}_{2}\tilde{\sigma}^{(N-0)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(s+1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N-0)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s)})+\tilde{C}_{2}\sum_{i=1}^{M^{(s)}}\sigma^{(s)}_{i,t}({\bm{z}}^{(s-1)},{\bm{x}}^{(s)})+\tilde{C}_{2}\tilde{\sigma}^{(N-1)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(s)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N-1)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s-1)})

(C.151)

\displaystyle\leq\tilde{C}_{2}\tilde{\sigma}^{(N-0)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(s+1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N-0)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s)})+\tilde{C}_{2}\sum_{i=1}^{M^{(s)}}\sigma^{(s)}_{i,t}({\bm{z}}^{(s-1)},{\bm{x}}^{(s)})+\tilde{C}^{2}_{2}\sum_{i=1}^{M^{(s)}}\sigma^{(s)}_{i,t}({\bm{z}}^{(s-1)},{\bm{x}}^{(s)})

(C.152)

\displaystyle\quad+\tilde{C}^{2}_{2}\tilde{\sigma}^{(N-1)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(s+1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N-1)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s)})+\tilde{C}^{2}_{2}\tilde{\sigma}^{(N-2)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(s)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N-2)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s-1)})

(C.153)

\displaystyle\leq

(C.154)

(C.155)

\displaystyle\leq(\tilde{C}_{2}+\tilde{C}^{2}_{2}+\cdots+\tilde{C}^{N-1}_{2})\tilde{\sigma}^{(N-0)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(s+1:N-0)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s)})+(\tilde{C}_{2}+\tilde{C}^{2}_{2}+\cdots+\tilde{C}^{N-1}_{2})\sum_{i=1}^{M^{(s)}}\sigma^{(s)}_{i,t}({\bm{z}}^{(s-1)},{\bm{x}}^{(s)})

(C.156)

\displaystyle\leq(N-1)\tilde{C}^{N-1}_{2}\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)}_{1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(s+1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s)})+(N-1)\tilde{C}^{N-1}_{2}\sum_{i=1}^{M^{(s)}}\sigma^{(s)}_{i,t}({\bm{z}}^{(s-1)},{\bm{x}}^{(s)}).

(C.157)

In addition,
(N-1)\tilde{C}^{N-1}_{2}
can be bounded by

\displaystyle(N-1)\tilde{C}^{N-1}_{2}

\displaystyle\leq N\tilde{C}^{N-1}_{2}

(C.158)

\displaystyle=N(4NM^{2}_{\text{prod}}M_{\text{sum}}C^{2N-2}_{0}C^{N}_{1})^{N-1}

(C.159)

\displaystyle\leq N(4NM^{2}_{\text{prod}}M_{\text{sum}}C^{N}_{1})^{N}C^{2N^{2}-4N+2}_{0}

(C.160)

\displaystyle\leq N(4NM^{2}_{\text{prod}}M_{\text{sum}}C^{N}_{1})^{N}C^{2N^{2}-4N+N}_{0}

(C.161)

\displaystyle=N(4NM^{2}_{\text{prod}}M_{\text{sum}}C_{0}^{2N-3}C^{N}_{1})^{N}

(C.162)

\displaystyle=NC^{N}_{2}=C_{3},

(C.163)

we get
lemma

C.13
.
∎

Lemma C.14
.

Assume that the same condition as in
theorem

C.10
holds.
Let
n\in[N]
and
{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)}\in\mathcal{Y}^{(n-1)}
.
Then, for any iteration
t\geq 1
and input
{\bm{x}}^{(n)}\in\mathcal{X}^{(n)}
, the following inequality holds:

\displaystyle\eta_{t}b^{(n)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})

\displaystyle\leq c^{(n)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})

(C.164)

\displaystyle\leq(2\beta^{1/2}+\eta_{t})b^{(n)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)}).

(C.165)

Proof.

From the definition of

c^{(n)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})
,
it is clear that
\eta_{t}b^{(n)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})\leq c^{(n)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})
.
On the other hand, from the definition of
\mathrm{UCB}^{(F)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})
, letting

(\tilde{\bm{x}}^{(n+1)},\ldots,\tilde{\bm{x}}^{(N)})=\argmax_{({\bm{x}}^{(n+1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)})}\Bigl(\tilde{\mu}^{(N)}_{1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(n:N)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})+\beta^{1/2}\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)}_{1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(n:N)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)}\Bigr)

(C.166)

we obtain

\mathrm{UCB}^{(F)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})=\tilde{\mu}^{(N)}_{1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)},\tilde{\bm{x}}^{(n+1)}\ldots,\tilde{\bm{x}}^{(N)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})+\beta^{1/2}\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)}_{1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)},\tilde{\bm{x}}^{(n+1)},\ldots,\tilde{\bm{x}}^{(N)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)}).

(C.167)

Similarly,
\mathrm{LCB}^{(F)}_{t}({\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})
can be bounded as follows:

\mathrm{LCB}^{(F)}_{t}({\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})\geq\tilde{\mu}^{(N)}_{1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)},\tilde{\bm{x}}^{(n+1)}\ldots,\tilde{\bm{x}}^{(N)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})-\beta^{1/2}\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)}_{1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)},\tilde{\bm{x}}^{(n+1)},\ldots,\tilde{\bm{x}}^{(N)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)}).

(C.168)

Hence, from the definition of

a^{(n)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})
and
b^{(n)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})
, we get

\displaystyle a^{(n)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})

\displaystyle\leq\mathrm{UCB}^{(F)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})-\mathrm{LCB}^{(F)}_{t}({\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})

(C.169)

\displaystyle\leq 2\beta^{1/2}\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)}_{1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)},\tilde{\bm{x}}^{(n+1)},\ldots,\tilde{\bm{x}}^{(N)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})

(C.170)

\displaystyle\leq 2\beta^{1/2}b^{(n)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)}).

(C.171)

Therefore,
c^{(n)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})
can be written as

\displaystyle c^{(n)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})

\displaystyle=\max\{a^{(n)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)}),\eta_{t}b^{(n)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})\}

(C.172)

\displaystyle\leq\max\{2\beta^{1/2}b^{(n)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)}),\eta_{t}b^{(n)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})\}

(C.173)

\displaystyle\leq(2\beta^{1/2}+\eta_{t})b^{(n)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)}).

(C.174)

∎

By using these lemmas, we prove
theorem

C.10
.

Proof.

Let
t\in N\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
.
Then, from
lemma

C.14
,
{\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1}
satisfies that

\displaystyle c^{(1)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1}|{\bm{0}})

\displaystyle\leq(2\beta^{1/2}+\eta_{t})b^{(1)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1}|{\bm{0}})

(C.175)

\displaystyle=(2\beta^{1/2}+\eta_{t})\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)}_{1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1},\tilde{\bm{x}}^{(2)},\ldots,\tilde{\bm{x}}^{(N)}|{\bm{0}}).

(C.176)

Thus, by combining
eqs.

C.176
and
C.13
,
c^{(1)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1}|{\bm{0}})
can be bounded as follows:

\displaystyle c^{(1)}_{t}

\displaystyle({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1}|{\bm{0}})

(C.177)

\displaystyle\leq(2\beta^{1/2}+\eta_{t})C_{3}\sum_{i=1}^{M^{(1)}}\sigma^{(1)}_{i,t}({\bm{0}},{\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1})+(2\beta^{1/2}+\eta_{t})C_{3}\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)}_{1,t}(\tilde{\bm{x}}^{(2)},\ldots,\tilde{\bm{x}}^{(N)}|{\bm{y}}^{(1)})

(C.178)

\displaystyle\leq(2\beta^{1/2}+\eta_{t})C_{3}\sum_{i=1}^{M^{(1)}}\sigma^{(1)}_{i,t}({\bm{0}},{\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1})+(2\beta^{1/2}+\eta_{t})C_{3}b^{(2)}_{t}(\tilde{\bm{x}}^{(2)}|{\bm{y}}^{(1)})

(C.179)

\displaystyle\leq(2\beta^{1/2}+\eta_{t})C_{3}\sum_{i=1}^{M^{(1)}}\sigma^{(1)}_{i,t}({\bm{0}},{\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1})+(2\beta^{1/2}+\eta_{t})C_{3}\eta^{-1}_{t}c^{(2)}_{t}(\tilde{\bm{x}}^{(2)}|{\bm{y}}^{(1)})

(C.180)

\displaystyle\leq(2\beta^{1/2}+\eta_{t})C_{3}\sum_{i=1}^{M^{(1)}}\sigma^{(1)}_{i,t}({\bm{0}},{\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1})+(2\beta^{1/2}+\eta_{t})C_{3}\eta^{-1}_{t}c^{(2)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(2)}_{t+2}|{\bm{y}}^{(1)})

(C.181)

\displaystyle\leq(2\beta^{1/2}+\eta_{t})C_{3}\sum_{i=1}^{M^{(1)}}\sigma^{(1)}_{i,t}({\bm{0}},{\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1})+(2\beta^{1/2}+\eta_{t})^{2}C_{3}\eta^{-1}_{t}b^{(2)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(2)}_{t+2}|{\bm{y}}^{(1)})

(C.182)

\displaystyle\leq(2\beta^{1/2}+\eta_{t})C_{3}\sum_{i=1}^{M^{(1)}}\sigma^{(1)}_{i,t}({\bm{0}},{\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1})+(2\beta^{1/2}+\eta_{t})^{2}C_{3}\eta^{-1}_{t}\cdot\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)}_{1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(2)}_{t+2},\tilde{\bm{x}}^{(3)},\ldots,\tilde{\bm{x}}^{(N)}|{\bm{y}}^{(1)}).

(C.183)

Hence, by using
lemma

C.13
again, we have

\displaystyle c^{(1)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1}|{\bm{0}})

\displaystyle\leq(2\beta^{1/2}+\eta_{t}+1)^{N}C^{N}_{3}\eta^{-N}_{t}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\sum_{i=1}^{M^{(n)}}\sigma^{(n)}_{i,t}({\bm{y}}^{(n-1)},{\bm{x}}^{(n)}_{t+n})

(C.184)

\displaystyle\leq(2\beta^{1/2}+1+1)^{N}C^{N}_{3}\eta^{-N}_{t}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\sum_{i=1}^{M^{(n)}}\sigma^{(n)}_{i,t}({\bm{y}}^{(n-1)},{\bm{x}}^{(n)}_{t+n})

(C.185)

\displaystyle=C_{4}\eta^{-N}_{t}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\sum_{i=1}^{M^{(n)}}\sigma^{(n)}_{i,t}({\bm{y}}^{(n-1)},{\bm{x}}^{(n)}_{t+n}).

(C.186)

This implies that

c^{(1)2}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1}|{\bm{0}})\leq C^{2}_{4}M_{\text{sum}}\eta^{-2N}_{t}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\sum_{i=1}^{M^{(n)}}\sigma^{(n)2}_{i,t}({\bm{y}}^{(n-1)},{\bm{x}}^{(n)}_{t+n}),

(C.187)

where the inequality is given by the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality.
Next, let
T\in N\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
and
K=T/N
. Then, the following inequality holds:

\displaystyle\sum_{t\in N\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}}^{KN}c^{(1)2}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1}|{\bm{0}})

\displaystyle\leq C^{2}_{4}M_{\text{sum}}\eta^{-2N}_{T}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\sum_{i=1}^{M^{(n)}}\sum_{t\in N\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}}^{T}\sigma^{(n)2}_{i,t}({\bm{y}}^{(n-1)},{\bm{x}}^{(n)}_{t+n})

(C.188)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{\log(1+\sigma^{-2})}C^{2}_{4}M_{\text{sum}}\eta^{-2N}_{T}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\sum_{i=1}^{M^{(n)}}\gamma_{T}

(C.189)

\displaystyle=\frac{2C^{2}_{4}M^{2}_{\text{sum}}}{\log(1+\sigma^{-2})}\gamma_{T}\eta^{-2N}_{T}.

(C.190)

Similarly, let

T^{\ast}=\argmin_{t\in N\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},t\leq T}c^{(1)2}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1}|{\bm{0}})
.
Then, we obtain

\displaystyle Kc^{(1)2}_{T^{\ast}}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{T^{\ast}+1}|{\bm{0}})

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t\in N\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}}^{KN}c^{(1)2}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1}|{\bm{0}})

(C.191)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2C^{2}_{4}M^{2}_{\text{sum}}}{\log(1+\sigma^{-2})}\gamma_{T}\eta^{-2N}_{T}.

(C.192)

This implies that

\displaystyle c^{(1)}_{T^{\ast}}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{T^{\ast}+1}|{\bm{0}})\leq\sqrt{\frac{2C^{2}_{4}M^{2}_{\text{sum}}}{\log(1+\sigma^{-2})}\gamma_{T}\eta^{-2N}_{T}K^{-1}}.

(C.193)

Furthermore, from the property of CIs and the definition of the estimated solution, the following inequalities hold:

\displaystyle F({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{\ast},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{\ast})

\displaystyle\leq\min_{t\in N\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},t\leq T}\mathrm{UCB}^{(F)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{\ast},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{\ast})

\displaystyle\leq\mathrm{UCB}^{(F)}_{T^{\ast}}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{\ast},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{\ast}),

\displaystyle F(\hat{\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{T},\ldots,\hat{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{T})

\displaystyle\geq\max_{t\in N\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},t\leq T}\mathrm{LCB}^{(F)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)})

\displaystyle\geq\mathrm{LCB}^{(F)}_{T^{\ast}}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{\ast},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{\ast}).

This implies that

\displaystyle F({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{\ast},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{\ast})-F(\hat{\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{T},\ldots,\hat{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{T})

\displaystyle\leq 2\beta^{1/2}\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)}_{1,T^{\ast}}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{\ast},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{\ast})

(C.194)

\displaystyle\leq 2\beta^{1/2}b^{(1)}_{T^{\ast}}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{\ast}|{\bm{0}})

(C.195)

\displaystyle\leq 2{\beta}^{1/2}\eta^{-1}_{T^{\ast}}c^{(1)}_{T^{\ast}}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{\ast}|{\bm{0}})\leq 2{\beta}^{1/2}\eta^{-1}_{T}c^{(1)}_{T^{\ast}}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{T^{\ast}+1}|{\bm{0}}).

(C.196)

Finally, noting that
K=T/N
, from
eq.

C.193
we obtain

\displaystyle F({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{\ast},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{\ast})-F(\hat{\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{T},\ldots,\hat{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{T})

\displaystyle\leq 2\beta^{1/2}\eta^{-1}_{T}\sqrt{\frac{2C^{2}_{4}M^{2}_{\text{sum}}}{\log(1+\sigma^{-2})}\gamma_{T}\eta^{-2N}_{T}K^{-1}}

(C.197)

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{8\beta C^{2}_{4}M^{2}_{\text{sum}}N}{\log(1+\sigma^{-2})}\gamma_{T}\eta^{-2N-2}_{T}T^{-1}}

(C.198)

\displaystyle<\sqrt{\xi^{2}}=\xi.

(C.199)

∎

D
Cascade Process Optimization Using CI-based AFs under Noisy Setting

In this section, we consider CI-based cascade process optimization methods with observation noise.
Hereafter, we assume that the observation noise
\epsilon^{(n)}_{m}
is a random variable with

\mathbb{E}[\epsilon^{(n)}_{m}]=0
and
-A\leq\epsilon^{(n)}_{m}\leq A
, where
A
is some positive constant.
In addition, we assume that

\epsilon^{(1)}_{1},\ldots,\epsilon^{(N)}_{M^{(N)}}
are mutually independent, and the distribution of the noise vector
{\bm{\epsilon}}=(\epsilon^{(1)}_{1},\ldots,\epsilon^{(N)}_{M^{(N)}})^{\top}
is known.
Finally, we also assume that noise vectors with respect to iteration
t
,
{\bm{\epsilon}}_{1},\ldots,{\bm{\epsilon}}_{t}
, are
independent and identically distributed random variables having the same distribution of
{\bm{\epsilon}}
.

Next, we define several notations.
For each
n\in[N]
, let
\tilde{\mathcal{Y}}^{(n)}(\supset\mathcal{Y}^{(n)})
be a set satisfying

\forall{\bm{w}}\in\tilde{\mathcal{Y}}^{(n-1)},\forall{\bm{x}}\in\mathcal{X}^{(n)},f^{(n)}({\bm{w}},{\bm{x}})+{\bm{\epsilon}}^{(n)}\in\tilde{\mathcal{Y}}^{(n)},

(D.1)

where
\tilde{\mathcal{Y}}^{(0)}=\{{\bm{0}}\}
and
{\bm{\epsilon}}^{(n)}=(\epsilon^{(n)}_{1},\ldots,{\epsilon}^{(n)}_{M^{(n)}})^{\top}
.
Note that

{\bm{z}}^{(n)}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n)})\in\mathcal{Y}^{(n)}\subset\tilde{\mathcal{Y}}^{(n)}
.
In addition, for any realization
{\bm{\epsilon}}
and input
{\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n)}
, we define
{\bm{z}}^{(n)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}}}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n)})
as

{\bm{z}}^{(n)}_{\bm{\epsilon}}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n)})=\begin{cases}{\bm{f}}^{(1)}({\bm{0}},{\bm{x}}^{(1)})+{\bm{\epsilon}}^{(1)}&(n=1),\\
{\bm{f}}^{(n)}({\bm{z}}^{(n-1)}_{\bm{\epsilon}}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n-1)}),{\bm{x}}^{(n)})+{\bm{\epsilon}}^{(n)}&(n\geq 2).\end{cases}

(D.2)

Furthermore, we define the function
G({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)})
as

G({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)})=\mathbb{E}_{\bm{\epsilon}}[{\bm{z}}^{(N)}_{\bm{\epsilon}}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n)})].

(D.3)

The function
G({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)})
is the expected value of the final-stage output with respect to
{\bm{\epsilon}}
when
({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)})
is used.
We emphasize that

F({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)})\neq G({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)})
in general.
Similarly, we define the optimal solution of each function as

\displaystyle({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{F,\ast},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{F,\ast})

\displaystyle=\argmax_{({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)})\in\mathcal{X}}F({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}),

(D.4)

\displaystyle({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{G,\ast},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{G,\ast})

\displaystyle=\argmax_{({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)})\in\mathcal{X}}G({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}).

(D.5)

By using these, for the selected input
{\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{t}
at iteration
t
, we define the expected regret
r_{G,t}
, cumulative expected regret
R_{G,T}
and simple expected regret
r^{(\text{S})}_{G,T}

as

\displaystyle r_{G,t}=G({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{G,\ast},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{G,\ast})-G({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{t}),

\displaystyle R_{G,t}=\sum_{t=1}^{T}r_{G_{t}},\quad r^{(\text{S})}_{G,T}=\min_{1\leq t\leq T}r_{G,t}.

We also define the regret

r_{F,t}
, cumulative regret
R_{F,T}
and simple regret
r^{(\text{S})}_{F,T}
as

\displaystyle r_{F,t}=F({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{F,\ast},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{F,\ast})-F({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{t}),

\displaystyle R_{F,t}=\sum_{t=1}^{T}r_{F_{t}},\quad r^{(\text{S})}_{F,T}=\min_{1\leq t\leq T}r_{F,t}.

Finally,
let
(\hat{\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{F,t},\ldots,\hat{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{F,t})

and

(\hat{\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{G,t},\ldots,\hat{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{G,t})

be respectively estimated solutions of
({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{F,\ast},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{F,\ast})

and
({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{G,\ast},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{G,\ast})

at iteration

t
.
Then, we define the regrets for estimated solutions,
\hat{r}_{F,t}
and
\hat{r}_{G,t}
, as

\displaystyle\hat{r}_{F,t}

\displaystyle=F({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{F,\ast},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{F,\ast})-F(\hat{\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{F,t},\ldots,\hat{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{F,t}),

(D.6)

\displaystyle\hat{r}_{G,t}

\displaystyle=G({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{G,\ast},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{G,\ast})-G(\hat{\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{G,t},\ldots,\hat{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{G,t}).

(D.7)

D.1
Credible Interval

In this section, we construct a valid CI under the noisy setting.
First, we introduce the following regularity assumption instead of
C.1
.

Assumption D.1

(Regularity assumption under noisy setting)
.

For each
n\in[N]
,
let
\tilde{\mathcal{Y}}^{(n-1)}\times\mathcal{X}^{(n)}
be a compact set, and let
\mathcal{H}_{k^{(n)}}
be an RKHS corresponding to the kernel
k^{(n)}
.
In addition, for each
n\in[N]
and
m\in[M^{(n)}]
, assume that
f^{(n)}_{m}\in\mathcal{H}_{k^{(n)}}

with
\|f^{(n)}_{m}\|_{{k^{(n)}}}\leq B
, where
B>0
is some constant.
Furthermore, assume that the observation noise
\epsilon^{(n)}_{m}
is a random variable
with

\mathbb{E}[\epsilon^{(n)}_{m}]=0
and
-A\leq\epsilon^{(n)}_{m}\leq A
, where
A
is some positive constant.
All elements of
{\bm{\epsilon}}=(\epsilon^{(1)}_{1},\ldots,\epsilon^{(N)}_{M^{(N)}})
are mutually independent, and

{\bm{\epsilon}}_{1},{\bm{\epsilon}}_{2},\ldots
are i.i.d. random variables having the same distribution of
{\bm{\epsilon}}
.

Then, the following lemma holds under the noisy setting.

Lemma D.2

(
Abbasi-Yadkori, 2012
)
.

Assume that
D.1
holds.
Let

\delta\in(0,1)
, and define

\beta^{(n)}_{t}=\Bigl(B+\frac{A}{\sigma}\sqrt{\log\operatorname{det}\left(\bm{I}_{L_{t}^{(n)}}+\sigma^{-2}\bm{K}^{(n)}_{t}\right)+2\log(1/\delta)}\Bigr)^{2}.

(D.8)

Then, for any
n\in[N]
and
m\in[M^{(n)}]
, the following inequality holds with probability at least
1-\delta
:

\left|f^{(n)}_{m}(\bm{w},\bm{x})-\mu^{(n)}_{m,t}(\bm{w},\bm{x})\right|\leq(\beta_{t}^{(n)})^{1/2}\sigma^{(n)}_{m,t}(\bm{w},\bm{x}),\;\forall\bm{w}\in\tilde{\mathcal{Y}}^{(n-1)},\;\forall\bm{x}\in\mathcal{X}^{(n)},\;\forall t\geq 1.

(D.9)

Proof.

From Theorem 3.11 of
Abbasi-Yadkori, 2012
, it is sufficient to show that

\epsilon^{(n)}_{m}
has
A
-sub-Gaussian property, i.e.,

\mathbb{E}[\exp(\lambda\epsilon^{(n)}_{m})]\leq\exp(\lambda^{2}A^{2}/2)\quad\forall\lambda\in\mathbb{R}.

(D.10)

Noting that
\epsilon^{(n)}_{m}
is a zero mean and bounded random variable, using
Hoeffding’s lemma
Massart, 2007
we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\exp(\lambda\epsilon^{(n)}_{m})]

\displaystyle\leq\exp(\lambda^{2}(A-(-A))^{2}/8)

(D.11)

\displaystyle=\exp(\lambda^{2}A^{2}/2)\quad\forall\lambda\in\mathbb{R}.

(D.12)

Thus,
\epsilon^{(n)}_{m}
has
A
-sub-Gaussian property
eq.

D.10
.
∎

From
eq.

D.9
, we have the following uniform bound.

Corollary D.3
.

Assume that
D.1
holds.
Let

\delta\in(0,1)
, and define

\displaystyle\beta^{(n)}_{t}

\displaystyle=\Bigl(B+\frac{A}{\sigma}\sqrt{\log\operatorname{det}\left(\bm{I}_{L_{t}^{(n)}}+\sigma^{-2}\bm{K}^{(n)}_{t}\right)+2\log(M_{\text{sum}}/\delta)}\Bigr)^{2},

(D.13)

\displaystyle\beta_{t}

\displaystyle=\max_{1\leq n\leq N,1\leq\tilde{t}\leq t}\beta^{(n)}_{\tilde{t}}.

(D.14)

Then, for any
n\in[N]
and
m\in[M^{(n)}],
the following inequality holds with probability at least
1-\delta
:

\left|f^{(n)}_{m}(\bm{w},\bm{x})-\mu^{(n)}_{m,t}(\bm{w},\bm{x})\right|\leq\beta_{t}^{1/2}\sigma^{(n)}_{m,t}(\bm{w},\bm{x}),\;\forall\bm{w}\in\tilde{\mathcal{Y}}^{(n-1)},\;\forall\bm{x}\in\mathcal{X}^{(n)},\;\forall t\geq 1.

(D.15)

From
corollary

D.3
,
we can also construct a valid CI for the
N
-stage cascade process under the noisy setting.
First, we construct CIs for
{\bm{z}}^{(n)}_{\bm{\epsilon}}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n)})
and

G({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)})
.
For any iteration
t\geq 1
, realization
{\bm{\epsilon}}
and input
({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n)})
, we define
\tilde{\bm{z}}^{(n)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n)})
as

\displaystyle\tilde{\bm{z}}^{(n)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n)})

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}{\bm{\epsilon}}^{(1)}+{\bm{\mu}}^{(1)}_{t}({\bm{0}},{\bm{x}}^{(1)})&(n=1),\\
{\bm{\epsilon}}^{(n)}+{\bm{\mu}}^{(n)}_{t}(\tilde{\bm{z}}^{(n-1)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n-1)}),{\bm{x}}^{(n)})&(n\geq 2).\end{cases}

(D.16)

Similarly, we define
\tilde{\sigma}^{(n)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},m,t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n)})
as

\displaystyle\tilde{\sigma}^{(n)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},m,t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n)})

\displaystyle={\sigma}^{(n)}_{m,t}(\tilde{\bm{z}}^{(n-1)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n-1)}),{\bm{x}}^{(n)})

(D.17)

\displaystyle\qquad+L_{f}\sum_{s=1}^{M^{(n-1)}}\tilde{\sigma}^{(n-1)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},s,t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n-1)}),

(D.18)

where
m\in[M^{(n)}]
and

\tilde{\sigma}^{(1)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},s,t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)})=\sigma^{(1)}_{s,t}({\bm{0}},{\bm{x}}^{(1)})
.
Then, the following holds.

Theorem D.4
.

Assume that
D.1
and
C.3
hold.
Also assume that
\tilde{\bm{z}}^{(n)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n)})\in\tilde{\mathcal{Y}}^{(n)}

for any
n\in[N]
, iteration
t\geq 1
, realization
{\bm{\epsilon}}
and input
({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n)})
.
Let
\delta\in(0,1)
, and define
\beta_{t}
by
D.14
.
Then, the following inequality holds with probability at least
1-\delta
:

\displaystyle|{z}^{(n)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},m}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n)})-\tilde{z}^{(n)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},m,t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n)})|\leq{\beta}^{1/2}_{t}\tilde{\sigma}^{(n)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},m,t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n)}),

(D.19)

\displaystyle\qquad\forall n\in[N],m\in[M^{(n)}],t\geq 1,{\bm{\epsilon}},({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n)}),

(D.20)

where
{z}^{(n)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},m}
and
\tilde{z}^{(n)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},m,t}
are the
m
-th element of

{\bm{z}}^{(n)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}}}
and
\tilde{\bm{z}}^{(n)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},t}
, respectively.

Proof.

By using the same argument as in the proof of
theorem

C.5
, we get
theorem

D.4
.
∎

From
theorem

D.4
, taking expectation with respect to
{\bm{\epsilon}}
, we get the following corollary.

Corollary D.5
.

Assume that the same condition as in
theorem

D.4
holds.
Let
\delta\in(0,1)
, and define
\beta_{t}
by
eq.

D.14
.
Then, with probability at least

1-\delta
, the following inequality holds for any
n\in[N]
,
m\in[M^{(n)}]
, iteration
t\geq 1
and input
{\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n)}
:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\bm{\epsilon}}[\tilde{z}^{(n)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},m,t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n)})-\beta^{1/2}_{t}\tilde{\sigma}^{(n)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},m,t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n)})]

(D.21)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{\bm{\epsilon}}[{z}^{(n)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},m}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n)})]

(D.22)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{\bm{\epsilon}}[\tilde{z}^{(n)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},m,t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n)})+\beta^{1/2}_{t}\tilde{\sigma}^{(n)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},m,t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n)})].

(D.23)

In particular, when
n=N
and
m=1
, it follows that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\bm{\epsilon}}[\tilde{z}^{(N)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)})-\beta^{1/2}_{t}\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)})]

(D.24)

\displaystyle\leq G({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)})

(D.25)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{\bm{\epsilon}}[\tilde{z}^{(N)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)})+\beta^{1/2}_{t}\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)})].

(D.26)

D.2
UCB-based Optimization Strategy for Expected Regrets

Here, we give a UCB-based AF and regret bounds for

R_{G,T}
and
r^{(\text{S})}_{G,T}
.
We define an
expected cascade process upper confidence bound (EcUCB) as

\mathrm{EcUCB}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)})=\mathbb{E}_{\bm{\epsilon}}[\tilde{z}^{(N)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)})+\beta^{1/2}_{t}\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)})].

(D.27)

By using this AF, we select the next evaluation point

({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{t+1})
by

\displaystyle({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{t+1})=\argmax_{({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)})\in\mathcal{X}}\mathrm{EcUCB}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}).

(D.28)

Moreover, let

\tilde{A}=\{{\bm{\tilde{}}{a}}_{1},\ldots,{\bm{\tilde{}}{a}}_{T}\}
be a subset of
\tilde{\mathcal{Y}}^{(n-1)}\times\mathcal{X}^{(n)}
,
and let

{\bm{y}}^{(n)}_{m,\tilde{A}}
be a random vector, where the
i
-th element of
{\bm{y}}^{(n)}_{m,\tilde{A}}
is given by
y^{(n)}_{m,{\bm{\tilde{}}{a}}_{i}}=f^{(n)}_{m}({\bm{\tilde{}}{a}}_{i})+\varepsilon^{(n)}_{{\bm{\tilde{}}{a}}_{i}}
. Then, the
maximum information gain
\tilde{\gamma}^{(n)}_{m,T}
at iteration

T
is given by

\displaystyle\tilde{\gamma}^{(n)}_{m,T}=\max_{\tilde{A}\subset\tilde{\mathcal{Y}}^{(n-1)}\times\mathcal{X}^{(n)},|\tilde{A}|=T}\mathrm{I}({\bm{y}}^{(n)}_{m,\tilde{A}};f^{(n)}_{m}).

(D.29)

Furthermore, we define
\tilde{\gamma}_{T}=\max_{1\leq n\leq N,1\leq m\leq M^{(n)}}\tilde{\gamma}^{(n)}_{m,T}
.
Then, the following theorem gives regret bounds for
R_{G,T}
and
r^{(\text{S})}_{G,T}
.

Theorem D.6
.

Assume that
D.1
,
C.3
and
C.4
hold.
Also assume that
\tilde{\bm{z}}^{(n)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n)})\in\tilde{\mathcal{Y}}^{(n)}

for any
n\in[N]
, iteration
t\geq 1
, realization
{\bm{\epsilon}}
and input
({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n)})
.
Let
\delta\in(0,1)
, and define
\beta_{t}
by
eq.

D.14
.
Then, when the optimization is performed using
D.28
,
the following holds:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}

\displaystyle\left(R_{G,T}\leq\sqrt{32M^{2}_{\text{prod}}M^{2}_{\text{sum}}T{C}^{2N}_{0,T}\left(\log(5M_{\text{sum}}/\delta)+\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{T}}{2\log(1+\sigma^{-2})}\right)}\quad\forall T\geq 1\right)\geq 1-2\delta,

(D.30)

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}

\displaystyle\left(r^{(\text{S})}_{G,T}\leq T^{-1/2}\sqrt{32M^{2}_{\text{prod}}M^{2}_{\text{sum}}{C}^{2N}_{0,T}\left(\log(5M_{\text{sum}}/\delta)+\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{T}}{2\log(1+\sigma^{-2})}\right)}\quad\forall T\geq 1\right)\geq 1-2\delta,

(D.31)

where

C_{0,t}=(1+L_{\sigma})\beta^{1/2}_{t}+L_{f}+1
.

Proof.

From
theorem

D.4
and the definition of
\tilde{\sigma}^{(n)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},m,t}(\cdot)
, noting that
{\sigma}^{(n)}_{m,t}(\cdot)
is Lipschitz continuity, the following inequality holds with probability at least
1-\delta
:

\displaystyle\tilde{\sigma}^{(n)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},m,t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n)})

(D.32)

\displaystyle=\sigma^{(n)}_{m,t}({\bm{z}}^{(n-1)}_{\bm{\epsilon}}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n-1)}),{\bm{x}}^{(n)})+L_{f}\sum_{s=1}^{M^{(n-1)}}\tilde{\sigma}^{(n-1)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},s,t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n-1)})

(D.33)

\displaystyle\quad+\sigma^{(n)}_{m,t}(\tilde{\bm{z}}^{(n-1)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n-1)}),{\bm{x}}^{(n)})-\sigma^{(n)}_{m,t}({\bm{z}}^{(n-1)}_{\bm{\epsilon}}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n-1)}),{\bm{x}}^{(n)})

(D.34)

\displaystyle\leq\sigma^{(n)}_{m,t}({\bm{z}}^{(n-1)}_{\bm{\epsilon}}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n-1)}),{\bm{x}}^{(n)})+L_{f}\sum_{s=1}^{M^{(n-1)}}\tilde{\sigma}^{(n-1)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},s,t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n-1)})

(D.35)

\displaystyle\quad+|\sigma^{(n)}_{m,t}({\bm{z}}^{(n-1)}_{\bm{\epsilon}}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n-1)}),{\bm{x}}^{(n)})-\sigma^{(n)}_{m,t}(\tilde{\bm{z}}^{(n-1)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n-1)}),{\bm{x}}^{(n)})|

(D.36)

\displaystyle\leq\sigma^{(n)}_{m,t}({\bm{z}}^{(n-1)}_{\bm{\epsilon}}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n-1)}),{\bm{x}}^{(n)})+L_{f}\sum_{s=1}^{M^{(n-1)}}\tilde{\sigma}^{(n-1)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},s,t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n-1)})

(D.37)

\displaystyle\quad+L_{\sigma}\sum_{s=1}^{M^{(n-1)}}|{z}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},s}^{(n-1)}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n-1)})-\tilde{z}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},s,t}^{(n-1)}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n-1)})|

(D.38)

\displaystyle\leq\sigma^{(n)}_{m,t}({\bm{z}}^{(n-1)}_{\bm{\epsilon}}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n-1)}),{\bm{x}}^{(n)})+L_{f}\sum_{s=1}^{M^{(n-1)}}\tilde{\sigma}^{(n-1)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},s,t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n-1)})

(D.39)

\displaystyle\quad+L_{\sigma}\beta^{1/2}_{t}\sum_{s=1}^{M^{(n-1)}}\tilde{\sigma}^{(n-1)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},s,t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n-1)})

(D.40)

\displaystyle=\sigma^{(n)}_{m,t}({\bm{z}}^{(n-1)}_{\bm{\epsilon}}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n-1)}),{\bm{x}}^{(n)})+(L_{\sigma}\beta^{1/2}_{t}+L_{f})\sum_{s=1}^{M^{(n-1)}}\tilde{\sigma}^{(n-1)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},s,t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n-1)})

(D.41)

\displaystyle\leq\sigma^{(n)}_{m,t}({\bm{z}}^{(n-1)}_{\bm{\epsilon}}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n-1)}),{\bm{x}}^{(n)})+{C}_{0,t}\sum_{s=1}^{M^{(n-1)}}\tilde{\sigma}^{(n-1)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},s,t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n-1)}).

(D.42)

Therefore, by repeating
eq.

D.42
we get

\displaystyle\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)})

(D.43)

\displaystyle\leq\sigma^{(N)}_{1,t}({\bm{z}}^{(N-1)}_{\bm{\epsilon}}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N-1)}),{\bm{x}}^{(N)})+{C}_{0,t}\sum_{s=1}^{M^{(N-1)}}\tilde{\sigma}^{(N-1)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},s,t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N-1)})

(D.44)

\displaystyle\leq\sigma^{(N)}_{1,t}({\bm{z}}^{(N-1)}_{\bm{\epsilon}}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N-1)}),{\bm{x}}^{(N)})+{C}_{0,t}\sum_{s=1}^{M^{(N-1)}}\sigma^{(N-1)}_{s,t}({\bm{z}}^{(N-2)}_{\bm{\epsilon}}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N-2)}),{\bm{x}}^{(N-1)})

(D.45)

\displaystyle\qquad+{C}^{2}_{0,t}M^{(N-1)}\sum_{u=1}^{M^{(N-2)}}\tilde{\sigma}^{(N-2)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},u,t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N-2)})

(D.46)

(D.47)

\displaystyle\leq{C}^{N-1}_{0,t}M_{\text{prod}}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\sum_{m=1}^{M^{(n)}}\sigma^{(n)}_{m,t}({\bm{z}}^{(n-1)}_{\bm{\epsilon}}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n-1)}),{\bm{x}}^{(n)}),

(D.48)

where
{\bm{z}}^{(0)}_{\bm{\epsilon}}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},{\bm{x}}^{(0)})={\bm{0}}
.
Hence, from the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality, it follows that

\displaystyle\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)2}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)})

(D.49)

\displaystyle\leq{C}^{2(N-1)}_{0,t}M^{2}_{\text{prod}}\left(\sum_{n=1}^{N}\sum_{m=1}^{M^{(n)}}1\right)\sum_{n=1}^{N}\sum_{m=1}^{M^{(n)}}\Bigl[\sigma^{(n)2}_{m,t}({\bm{z}}^{(n-1)}_{\bm{\epsilon}}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n-1)}),{\bm{x}}^{(n)})\Bigr]

(D.50)

\displaystyle={C}^{2(N-1)}_{0,t}M^{2}_{\text{prod}}M_{\text{sum}}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\sum_{m=1}^{M^{(n)}}\Bigl[\sigma^{(n)2}_{m,t}({\bm{z}}^{(n-1)}_{\bm{\epsilon}}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n-1)}),{\bm{x}}^{(n)})\Bigr].

(D.51)

Next, from
corollary

D.5
and the selection rule
eq.

D.28
, the following holds:

\displaystyle G({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{G,\ast},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{G,\ast})

\displaystyle\leq\mathrm{EcUCB}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{G,\ast},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{G,\ast})

(D.52)

\displaystyle\leq\mathrm{EcUCB}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{t+1})

(D.53)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{\bm{\epsilon}}[\tilde{z}^{(N)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{t+1})+\beta^{1/2}_{t}\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{t+1})].

(D.54)

Similarly, since
G({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{t+1})
satisfies that

G({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{t+1})\geq\mathbb{E}_{\bm{\epsilon}}[\tilde{z}^{(N)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{t+1})\beta^{1/2}_{t}\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{t+1})],

(D.55)

the regret
r_{G,t}
can be bounded as follows:

\displaystyle r_{G,t}

\displaystyle=G({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{G,\ast},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{G,\ast})-G({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{t+1})

(D.56)

\displaystyle\leq 2\beta^{1/2}_{t}\mathbb{E}_{\bm{\epsilon}}[\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{t+1})].

(D.57)

Therefore, by using
eq.

D.57
,
R^{2}_{G,T}
can be written as

\displaystyle R^{2}_{G,T}

\displaystyle=\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}r_{G,t}\right)^{2}

(D.58)

\displaystyle\leq T\sum_{t=1}^{T}r^{2}_{G,t}

(D.59)

\displaystyle\leq T\sum_{t=1}^{T}4\beta_{t}(\mathbb{E}_{\bm{\epsilon}}[\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{t+1})])^{2}

(D.60)

\displaystyle\leq T\sum_{t=1}^{T}4\beta_{t}\mathbb{E}_{\bm{\epsilon}}[\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)2}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{t+1})],

(D.61)

where the first inequality is given by the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality, and the last inequality is given by Jensen’s inequality.
Thus, by substituting
eq.

D.51
into
eq.

D.61
, we obtain

R^{2}_{G,T}\leq 4T\beta_{T}\tilde{C}^{2(N-1)}_{T}M^{2}_{\text{prod}}M_{\text{sum}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{\bm{\epsilon}}[S_{{\bm{\epsilon}},t}],

(D.62)

where
S_{{\bm{\epsilon}},t}
is given by

S_{{\bm{\epsilon}},t}=\sum_{n=1}^{N}\sum_{m=1}^{M^{(n)}}\sigma^{(n)2}_{m,t}({\bm{z}}^{(n-1)}_{\bm{\epsilon}}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n-1)}_{t+1}),{\bm{x}}^{(n)}_{t+1}).

(D.63)

Here,
since
k^{(n)}(\cdot,\cdot)\leq 1
, the random variable

S_{{\bm{\epsilon}},t}
satisfies

0\leq S_{{\bm{\epsilon}},t}\leq M_{\text{sum}}
.
Hence, from Lemma 3 of
Kirschner and Krause, 2018
, the following holds with probability at least
1-\delta
:

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{\bm{\epsilon}}[S_{{\bm{\epsilon}},t}]

\displaystyle\leq 2\sum_{t=1}^{T}S_{{\bm{\epsilon}}_{t+1},t}+4M_{\text{sum}}\log(1/\delta)+8M_{\text{sum}}\log(4M_{\text{sum}})+1

(D.64)

\displaystyle\leq 2\sum_{t=1}^{T}S_{{\bm{\epsilon}}_{t+1},t}+8M_{\text{sum}}\log(1/\delta)+8M_{\text{sum}}\log(4M_{\text{sum}})+8M_{\text{sum}}\log 1.25

(D.65)

\displaystyle=2\sum_{t=1}^{T}S_{{\bm{\epsilon}}_{t+1},t}+8M_{\text{sum}}\log(5M_{\text{sum}}/\delta).

(D.66)

Therefore, by combining
eqs.

D.62
and
D.66
, we have

\displaystyle R^{2}_{G,T}

\displaystyle\leq 32T\beta_{T}{C}^{2(N-1)}_{T,0}M^{2}_{\text{prod}}M^{2}_{\text{sum}}\log(5M_{\text{sum}}/\delta)

(D.67)

\displaystyle\qquad+8T\beta_{T}{C}^{2(N-1)}_{0,T}M^{2}_{\text{prod}}M_{\text{sum}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\sum_{m=1}^{M^{(n)}}\Bigl[\sigma^{(n)2}_{m,t}({\bm{z}}^{(n-1)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}}_{t+1}}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n-1)}_{t+1}),{\bm{x}}^{(n)}_{t+1})\Bigr].

(D.68)

Furthermore, by using the same argument as in Lemma 5.3 and 5.4 of
Srinivas et al., 2010
, we get

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sigma^{(n)2}_{m,t}({\bm{z}}^{(n-1)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}}_{t+1}}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n-1)}_{t+1}),{\bm{x}}^{(n)}_{t+1})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{\log(1+\sigma^{-2})}\tilde{\gamma}^{(n)}_{m,T}

(D.69)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{\log(1+\sigma^{-2})}\tilde{\gamma}_{T}.

(D.70)

Hence, from
eq.

D.68
and
eq.

D.70
, noting that
\beta_{T}\leq{C}^{2}_{0,T}
we obtain

\displaystyle R^{2}_{G,T}

\displaystyle\leq 32T\beta_{T}{C}^{2(N-1)}_{0,T}M^{2}_{\text{prod}}M^{2}_{\text{sum}}\log(5M_{\text{sum}}/\delta)+\frac{16}{\log(1+\sigma^{-2})}T\beta_{T}{C}^{2(N-1)}_{0,T}M^{2}_{\text{prod}}M^{2}_{\text{sum}}\tilde{\gamma}_{T}

(D.71)

\displaystyle=32T\beta_{T}{C}^{2(N-1)}_{0,T}M^{2}_{\text{prod}}M^{2}_{\text{sum}}\left(\log(5M_{\text{sum}}/\delta)+\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{T}}{2\log(1+\sigma^{-2})}\right)

(D.72)

\displaystyle\leq 32T{C}^{2N}_{0,T}M^{2}_{\text{prod}}M^{2}_{\text{sum}}\left(\log(5M_{\text{sum}}/\delta)+\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{T}}{2\log(1+\sigma^{-2})}\right).

(D.73)

Therefore, with probability at least
1-2\delta
,
R_{G,T}
can be bounded as follows:

R_{G,T}\leq\sqrt{32M^{2}_{\text{prod}}M^{2}_{\text{sum}}T{C}^{2N}_{0,T}\left(\log(5M_{\text{sum}}/\delta)+\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{T}}{2\log(1+\sigma^{-2})}\right).}

(D.74)

Similarly, from the definition of

r^{(\text{S})}_{G,T}
, it follows that

\displaystyle Tr^{(\text{S})}_{G,T}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t=1}^{T}r_{G,t}=R_{G,T}

(D.75)

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{32M^{2}_{\text{prod}}M^{2}_{\text{sum}}T{C}^{2N}_{0,T}\left(\log(5M_{\text{sum}}/\delta)+\frac{\tilde{\gamma}_{T}}{2\log(1+\sigma^{-2})}\right)}.

(D.76)

∎

D.3
Optimistic Improvement-based AF for the Expectation of the Final Stage Output

We give an optimistic improvement-based AF for
G
under the noisy setting.
Let
s\in\{0,\ldots,N-1\}
and
{\bm{y}}\in\tilde{\mathcal{Y}}^{(s)}
.
Then, we define

{\bm{z}}^{(n)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}}}(\cdot|{\bm{y}})
,
\tilde{\bm{z}}^{(n)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},t}(\cdot|{\bm{y}})
and
\tilde{\sigma}^{(n)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},m,t}(\cdot|{\bm{y}})
as

\displaystyle{\bm{z}}^{(n)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}}}({\bm{x}}^{(s+1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}})=

\displaystyle\begin{cases}{\bm{\epsilon}}^{(s+1)}+{\bm{f}}^{(s+1)}({\bm{y}},{\bm{x}}^{(s+1)})&(n=s+1),\\
{\bm{\epsilon}}^{(n)}+{\bm{f}}^{(n)}({\bm{z}}^{(n-1)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}}}({\bm{x}}^{(s+1:n-1)}|{\bm{y}}),{\bm{x}}^{(n)})&(n\geq s+2),\end{cases}

(D.77)

\displaystyle\tilde{\bm{z}}^{(n)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},t}({\bm{x}}^{(s+1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}})=

\displaystyle\begin{cases}{\bm{\epsilon}}^{(s+1)}+{\bm{\mu}}^{(s+1)}_{t}({\bm{y}},{\bm{x}}^{(s+1)})&(n=s+1),\\
{\bm{\epsilon}}^{(n)}+{\bm{\mu}}^{(n)}_{t}(\tilde{\bm{z}}^{(n-1)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},t}({\bm{x}}^{(s+1:n-1)}|{\bm{y}}),{\bm{x}}^{(n)})&(n\geq s+2),\end{cases}

(D.78)

\displaystyle\tilde{\sigma}^{(n)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},m,t}({\bm{x}}^{(s+1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}})

\displaystyle={\sigma}^{(n)}_{m,t}(\tilde{\bm{z}}^{(n-1)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},t}({\bm{x}}^{(s+1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n-1)}|{\bm{y}}),{\bm{x}}^{(n)})

(D.79)

\displaystyle\quad+L_{f}\sum_{s=1}^{M^{(n-1)}}\tilde{\sigma}^{(n-1)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},s,t}({\bm{x}}^{(s+1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n-1)}|{\bm{y}}),

(D.80)

where

\tilde{\sigma}^{(s)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},m,t}({\bm{x}}^{(s+1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(s)}|{\bm{y}})=0
and

{\bm{z}}^{(s)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}}}({\bm{x}}^{(s+1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(s)}|{\bm{y}})=\tilde{\bm{z}}^{(s)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},t}({\bm{x}}^{(s+1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(s)}|{\bm{y}})={\bm{y}}
.
Then, the following theorem holds.

Theorem D.7
.

Assume that
D.1
and
C.3
hold.
Also assume that
\tilde{\bm{z}}^{(n)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},t}({\bm{x}}^{(s+1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}})\in\tilde{\mathcal{Y}}^{(n)}

for any

s\in\{0,\ldots,N-1\}
,
n\in\{s+1,\ldots,N\}
, iteration
t\geq 1
, realization
{\bm{\epsilon}}
, given
{\bm{y}}\in\tilde{\mathcal{Y}}^{(s)}
and input
({\bm{x}}^{(s+1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n)})
.
Let
\delta\in(0,1)
, and define
\beta_{t}
by
eq.

D.14
.
Then, the following inequality holds with probability at least
1-\delta
:

|{z}^{(n)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},m}({\bm{x}}^{(s+1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}})-\tilde{z}^{(n)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},m,t}({\bm{x}}^{(s+1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}})|\leq{\beta}^{1/2}_{t}\tilde{\sigma}^{(n)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},m,t}({\bm{x}}^{(s+1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}}),

(D.81)

where
m\in[M^{(n)}]
, and
{z}^{(n)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},m}(\cdot|{\bm{y}})
and
\tilde{z}^{(n)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},m,t}(\cdot|{\bm{y}})
are the
m
-th element of

{\bm{z}}^{(n)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}}}(\cdot|{\bm{y}})
and
\tilde{\bm{z}}^{(n)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},t}(\cdot|{\bm{y}})
, respectively.

Proof.

By using the same argument as in the proof of
theorem

C.5
, we have
theorem

D.7
.
∎

From
theorem

D.7
, taking expectation with respect to
{\bm{\epsilon}}
, we get the following corollary.

Corollary D.8
.

Assume that the same condition as in
theorem

D.7
holds.
Let
\delta\in(0,1)
, and define
\beta_{t}
by
eq.

D.14
.
Then, the following inequality holds with probability at least
1-\delta
:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{\epsilon}}|{\bm{y}}}[\tilde{z}^{(n)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},m,t}({\bm{x}}^{(s+1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}})-{\beta}^{1/2}_{t}\tilde{\sigma}^{(n)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},m,t}({\bm{x}}^{(s+1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}})]

(D.82)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{\epsilon}}|{\bm{y}}}[{z}^{(n)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},m}({\bm{x}}^{(s+1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}})]

(D.83)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{\epsilon}}|{\bm{y}}}[\tilde{z}^{(n)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},m,t}({\bm{x}}^{(s+1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}})+{\beta}^{1/2}_{t}\tilde{\sigma}^{(n)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},m,t}({\bm{x}}^{(s+1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}})],

(D.84)

where
\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{\epsilon}}|{\bm{y}}}[\cdot]
is the conditional expectation of
(\cdot)
given
{\bm{y}}
.

Based on this lemma, we give valid AFs.
Let
n\in[N]
and
{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)}\in\tilde{\mathcal{Y}}^{(n-1)}
.
Then, for any
{\bm{x}}^{(n)}\in\mathcal{X}^{(n)}
and iteration
t\geq 1
, we define the optimistic maximum value at the final stage under given
{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)}
,

\mathrm{UCB}^{(G)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})
, as

\displaystyle\mathrm{UCB}^{(G)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})=

(D.85)

\displaystyle\max_{({\bm{x}}^{(n+1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)})}\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{\epsilon}}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)}}\Bigl[(\tilde{{z}}^{(N)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})+\beta^{1/2}_{t}\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)}))\Bigr],

(D.86)

where the max operator is ignored when
n=N
.
Similarly, we define the pessimistic maximum value at the final stage under given
{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)}
,

\mathrm{LCB}^{(G)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})
, as

\displaystyle\mathrm{LCB}^{(G)}_{t}({\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})

(D.87)

\displaystyle=\max_{({\bm{x}}^{(n)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)})}\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{\epsilon}}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)}}\Bigl[(\tilde{z}^{(N)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})-\beta^{1/2}_{t}\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)}))\Bigr].

(D.88)

Moreover, for each
T\geq 1
, we define the pessimistic maximum value at the final stage as

Q_{T}=\max_{({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)})}\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{\epsilon}}}\Bigl[(\tilde{z}^{(N)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},1,T}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)})-\beta^{1/2}_{T}\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},1,T}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}))\Bigr].

(D.89)

Then, we define the pessimistic improvement for the final stage with respect to
{\bm{x}}^{(n)}
by

\tilde{a}^{(n)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})=\mathrm{UCB}^{(G)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})-\max\{\mathrm{LCB}^{(G)}_{t}({\bm{y}}^{(n-1)}),Q_{t+n-1}\}.

(D.90)

We also define the maximum uncertainty for the final stage with respect to
{\bm{x}}^{(n)}
as

\displaystyle\tilde{b}^{(n)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})=\max_{({\bm{x}}^{(n+1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)})}\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{\epsilon}}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)}}[\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})].

(D.91)

Then, we give the AF

\tilde{c}^{(n)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})
by

\tilde{c}^{(n)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})=\max\Bigl\{\tilde{a}^{(n)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)}),\eta_{t}\tilde{b}^{(n)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})\Bigr\},

(D.92)

where
\eta_{t}
is a given learning rate.
Furthermore, we select the next point
{\bm{x}}^{(n)}_{t+n}
by

\displaystyle{\bm{x}}^{(n)}_{t+n}

\displaystyle=\argmax_{{\bm{x}}^{(n)}\in\mathcal{X}^{(n)}}\tilde{c}^{(n)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)}_{t+n-1}),

(D.93)

\displaystyle{\bm{y}}^{(n)}_{t+n}

\displaystyle={\bm{f}}^{(n)}({\bm{y}}^{(n-1)}_{t+n-1},{\bm{x}}^{(n)}_{t+n})+{\bm{\epsilon}}^{(n)}_{t+n},

(D.94)

where
{\bm{y}}^{(0)}_{t}={\bm{0}}
.
Finally, we define the estimated solution
(\hat{\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{G,T},\ldots,\hat{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{G,T})

by using the pessimistic maximum value as follows:

\displaystyle(\hat{\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{G,T},\ldots,\hat{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{G,T})=

(D.95)

\displaystyle\argmax_{({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)})\in\mathcal{X},1\leq t\leq T}\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{\epsilon}}}\Bigl[(\tilde{z}^{(N)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)})-\beta^{1/2}_{t}\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}))\Bigr].

(D.96)

Then, the following theorem holds.

Theorem D.9
.

Assume that
D.1
,
C.3
and
C.4
hold.
Also assume that
\tilde{\bm{z}}^{(n)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},t}({\bm{x}}^{(s+1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}})\in\tilde{\mathcal{Y}}^{(n)}

for any

s\in\{0,\ldots,N-1\}
,
n\in\{s+1,\ldots,N\}
, iteration
t\geq 1
, realization
{\bm{\epsilon}}
, given
{\bm{y}}\in\tilde{\mathcal{Y}}^{(s)}
and input
({\bm{x}}^{(s+1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(n)})
.
Let
\delta\in(0,1)
and
\xi>0
, and define
\beta_{t}
by
eq.

D.14
and
\eta_{t}=(1+\log t)^{-1}
.
Then, when the optimization is performed using
eq.

D.94
, the following inequality holds with probability at least

1-(N+1)\delta
:

G({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{G,\ast},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{G,\ast})-G(\hat{\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{G,T},\ldots,\hat{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{G,T})<\xi,

(D.97)

where
T
is the smallest positive integer satisfying
T\in N\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
and

\frac{N}{T}\sqrt{C^{2(N+2)}_{6,T}(C_{7}+C_{8}T\tilde{\gamma}_{T})}<\xi.

(D.98)

Here,
C_{6,t}
,
C_{7}
and
C_{8}
are given by

C_{2,t}=4NM^{2}_{\text{prod}}M_{\text{sum}}C^{2N-2}_{0,t}C^{N}_{1},\ C_{3,t}=NC_{2,t}^{N},\ C_{6,t}=2C_{3,t}\eta^{-1}_{t}(2\beta^{1/2}_{t}+2),C_{5}=(1+L_{f})^{N}M_{\text{prod}}M_{\text{sum}},\ C_{7}=2\left(8C_{5}\log\frac{5C_{5}}{\delta}N\right)^{2},\ C_{8}=\frac{4M^{2}_{\text{sum}}}{\log(1+\sigma^{-2})}.

In order to prove
theorem

D.9
, we give two lemmas.

Lemma D.10
.

Assume that the same condition as in
theorem

D.4
holds.
Then, the following holds with probability at least
1-\delta
:

\displaystyle\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(s)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s-1)}_{\bm{\epsilon}})

\displaystyle\leq C_{3,t}\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(s+1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}|{\bm{z}}^{(s)}_{\bm{\epsilon}})+C_{3,t}\sum_{i=1}^{M^{(s)}}\sigma^{(s)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},i,t}({\bm{z}}^{(s-1)}_{\bm{\epsilon}},{\bm{x}}^{(s)}).

(D.99)

Proof.

By using the same argument as in
lemma

C.13
, we have
lemma

D.10
.
∎

Lemma D.11
.

Assume that the same condition as in
theorem

D.4
holds.
Then, the following inequality holds:

\eta_{t}\tilde{b}^{(n)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})\leq\tilde{c}^{(n)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})\leq(2\beta^{1/2}_{t}+\eta_{t})\tilde{b}^{(n)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)}).

(D.100)

Proof.

By using the same argument as in
lemma

C.14
, we get
lemma

D.11
.
∎

By using these lemmas, we prove
theorem

D.9
.

Proof.

From
lemma

D.11
, the following holds:

\displaystyle\tilde{c}^{(1)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1}|{\bm{0}})

\displaystyle\leq(2\beta^{1/2}_{t}+\eta_{t})\tilde{b}^{(1)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1}~{\bm{0}})

(D.101)

\displaystyle=(2\beta^{1/2}_{t}+\eta_{t})\mathbb{E}_{\bm{\epsilon}}[\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1},\hat{\bm{x}}^{(2)}_{t+1},\ldots,\hat{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{t+1}|{\bm{0}})].

(D.102)

In addition, for the positive integer
NK\equiv T\in N\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}
satisfying the theorem’s inequality,

\tilde{c}^{(1)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1}|{\bm{0}})
satisfies that

\displaystyle\sum_{t\in N\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}}^{T}\tilde{c}^{(1)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1}|{\bm{0}})

\displaystyle\leq(2\beta^{1/2}_{T}+2)\sum_{t\in N\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{\bm{\epsilon}}[\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1},\hat{\bm{x}}^{(2)}_{t+1},\ldots,\hat{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{t+1}|{\bm{0}})]

(D.103)

\displaystyle=(2\beta^{1/2}_{T}+2)\sum_{t\in N\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{\epsilon}}^{(1)}}[\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{\epsilon}}|{\bm{\epsilon}}^{(1)}}\Bigl[\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1},\hat{\bm{x}}^{(2)}_{t+1},\ldots,\hat{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{t+1}|{\bm{0}})|{\bm{\epsilon}}^{(1)}]\Bigr].

(D.104)

Here, the conditional expectation
\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{\epsilon}}|{\bm{\epsilon}}^{(1)}}[\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1},\hat{\bm{x}}^{(2)}_{t+1},\ldots,\hat{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{t+1}|{\bm{0}})|{\bm{\epsilon}}^{(1)}]
is
a non-negative random variable with respect to

{\bm{\epsilon}}^{(1)}
, and satisfies that

\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{\epsilon}}|{\bm{\epsilon}}^{(1)}}[\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1},\hat{\bm{x}}^{(2)}_{t+1},\ldots,\hat{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{t+1}|{\bm{0}})|{\bm{\epsilon}}^{(1)}]\leq(1+L_{f})^{N}M_{\text{prod}}M_{\text{sum}}=C_{5},

(D.105)

where the inequality is given by
k^{(n)}(\cdot,\cdot)\leq 1
.
Hence, from Lemma 3 of
Kirschner and Krause, 2018
, the following holds with probability at least
1-\delta
:

\displaystyle\sum_{t\in N\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{\epsilon}}^{(1)}}[\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{\epsilon}}|{\bm{\epsilon}}^{(1)}}[\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1},\hat{\bm{x}}^{(2)}_{t+1},\ldots,\hat{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{t+1}|{\bm{0}})|{\bm{\epsilon}}^{(1)}]]

(D.106)

\displaystyle\leq 4C_{5}\log\frac{1}{\delta}+8C_{5}\log(4C_{5})+1+2\sum_{t\in N\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{\epsilon}}|{\bm{\epsilon}}^{(1)}_{t}}[\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1},\hat{\bm{x}}^{(2)}_{t+1},\ldots,\hat{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{t+1}|{\bm{0}})|{\bm{\epsilon}}^{(1)}_{t}]

(D.107)

\displaystyle\leq 8C_{5}\log\frac{5C_{5}}{\delta}+2\sum_{t\in N\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{\epsilon}}|{\bm{\epsilon}}^{(1)}_{t}}[\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1},\hat{\bm{x}}^{(2)}_{t+1},\ldots,\hat{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{t+1}|{\bm{0}})|{\bm{\epsilon}}^{(1)}_{t}].

(D.108)

Moreover, from
lemma

D.10
, with probability at least
1-\delta
the following inequality holds uniformly:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{\epsilon}}|{\bm{\epsilon}}^{(1)}_{t}}[\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1},\hat{\bm{x}}^{(2)}_{t+1},\ldots,\hat{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{t+1}|{\bm{0}})|{\bm{\epsilon}}^{(1)}_{t}]

(D.109)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{\epsilon}}|{\bm{\epsilon}}^{(1)}_{t}}[C_{3,T}\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},1,t}(\hat{\bm{x}}^{(2)}_{t+1},\ldots,\hat{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{t+1}|{\bm{y}}^{(1)}_{t+1})]+C_{3,T}\sum_{i=1}^{M^{(1)}}\sigma^{(1)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},1,t}({\bm{0}},{\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1}).

(D.110)

Therefore, it follows that

\displaystyle\sum_{t\in N\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{\epsilon}}^{(1)}}[\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{\epsilon}}|{\bm{\epsilon}}^{(1)}}[\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1},\hat{\bm{x}}^{(2)}_{t+1},\ldots,\hat{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{t+1}|{\bm{0}})|{\bm{\epsilon}}^{(1)}]]

(D.111)

\displaystyle\leq 8C_{5}\log\frac{5C_{5}}{\delta}+2\sum_{t\in N\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{\epsilon}}|{\bm{\epsilon}}^{(1)}_{t}}[\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1},\hat{\bm{x}}^{(2)}_{t+1},\ldots,\hat{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{t+1}|{\bm{0}})|{\bm{\epsilon}}^{(1)}_{t}]

(D.112)

\displaystyle\leq 8C_{5}\log\frac{5C_{5}}{\delta}+2C_{3,T}\sum_{t\in N\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}}^{T}\sum_{i=1}^{M^{(1)}}\sigma^{(1)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},1,t}({\bm{0}},{\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1})+2C_{3,T}\sum_{t\in N\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{\epsilon}}|{\bm{\epsilon}}^{(1)}_{t}}[\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},1,t}(\hat{\bm{x}}^{(2)}_{t+1},\ldots,\hat{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{t+1}|{\bm{y}}^{(1)}_{t+1})]

(D.113)

\displaystyle\leq 8C_{5}\log\frac{5C_{5}}{\delta}+2C_{3,T}\sum_{t\in N\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}}^{T}\sum_{i=1}^{M^{(1)}}\sigma^{(1)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},1,t}({\bm{0}},{\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1})+2C_{3,T}\eta^{-1}_{T}\sum_{t\in N\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}}^{T}\eta_{t}\tilde{b}^{(2)}_{t}(\hat{\bm{x}}^{(2)}_{t+1}|{\bm{y}}^{(1)}_{t+1})

(D.114)

\displaystyle\leq 8C_{5}\log\frac{5C_{5}}{\delta}+2C_{3,T}\sum_{t\in N\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}}^{T}\sum_{i=1}^{M^{(1)}}\sigma^{(1)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},1,t}({\bm{0}},{\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1})+2C_{3,T}\eta^{-1}_{T}\sum_{t\in N\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}}^{T}\tilde{c}^{(2)}_{t}(\hat{\bm{x}}^{(2)}_{t+1}|{\bm{y}}^{(1)}_{t+1})

(D.115)

\displaystyle\leq 8C_{5}\log\frac{5C_{5}}{\delta}+2C_{3,T}\sum_{t\in N\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}}^{T}\sum_{i=1}^{M^{(1)}}\sigma^{(1)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},1,t}({\bm{0}},{\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1})+2C_{3,T}\eta^{-1}_{T}\sum_{t\in N\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}}^{T}\tilde{c}^{(2)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(2)}_{t+2}|{\bm{y}}^{(1)}_{t+1})

(D.116)

\displaystyle\leq 8C_{5}\log\frac{5C_{5}}{\delta}+2C_{3,T}\sum_{t\in N\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}}^{T}\sum_{i=1}^{M^{(1)}}\sigma^{(1)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},1,t}({\bm{0}},{\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1})+2C_{3,T}\eta^{-1}_{T}(2\beta^{1/2}_{t}+2)\sum_{t\in N\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}}^{T}\tilde{b}^{(2)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(2)}_{t+2}|{\bm{y}}^{(1)}_{t+1})

(D.117)

\displaystyle\leq 8C_{5}\log\frac{5C_{5}}{\delta}+2C_{3,T}\sum_{t\in N\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}}^{T}\sum_{i=1}^{M^{(1)}}\sigma^{(1)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},1,t}({\bm{0}},{\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1})+2C_{3,T}\eta^{-1}_{T}(2\beta^{1/2}_{t}+2)

(D.118)

\displaystyle\qquad\cdot\sum_{t\in N\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{\epsilon}}^{(2)}}\Bigl[\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{\epsilon}}|{\bm{y}}^{(1)}_{t+1},{\bm{\epsilon}}^{(2)}}\Bigl[\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(2)}_{t+2},\hat{\bm{x}}^{(3)}_{t+2},\ldots,\hat{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{t+2}|{\bm{y}}^{(1)}_{t+1})|{\bm{\epsilon}}^{(2)}\Bigr]\Bigr]

(D.119)

\displaystyle\leq 8C_{5}\log\frac{5C_{5}}{\delta}+C_{6,T}\sum_{t\in N\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}}^{T}\sum_{i=1}^{M^{(1)}}\sigma^{(1)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},1,t}({\bm{0}},{\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1})

(D.120)

\displaystyle\quad+C_{6,T}\sum_{t\in N\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{\epsilon}}^{(2)}}\Bigl[\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{\epsilon}}|{\bm{y}}^{(1)}_{t+1},{\bm{\epsilon}}^{(2)}}\Bigl[\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(2)}_{t+2},\hat{\bm{x}}^{(3)}_{t+2},\ldots,\hat{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{t+2}|{\bm{y}}^{(1)}_{t+1})|{\bm{\epsilon}}^{(2)}\Bigr]\Bigr].

(D.121)

By repeating this process, with probability at least
1-(N+1)\delta
, the following holds:

\displaystyle\sum_{t\in N\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{\epsilon}}^{(1)}}[\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{\epsilon}}|{\bm{\epsilon}}^{(1)}}[\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1},\hat{\bm{x}}^{(2)}_{t+1},\ldots,\hat{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{t+1}|{\bm{0}})|{\bm{\epsilon}}^{(1)}]]

(D.122)

\displaystyle\leq 8C_{5}\log\frac{5C_{5}}{\delta}NC_{6,T}^{N}+C_{6,T}^{N}\sum_{t\in N\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}}^{T}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\sum_{i=1}^{M^{(n)}}\sigma^{(n)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},i,t}({\bm{y}}^{(n-1)}_{t+n-1},{\bm{x}}^{(n)}_{t+n}).

(D.123)

By combining this and

\displaystyle\sum_{t\in N\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}}^{T}\tilde{c}^{(1)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1}|{\bm{0}})

\displaystyle\leq(2\beta^{1/2}_{T}+2)\sum_{t\in N\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{\epsilon}}^{(1)}}[\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{\epsilon}}|{\bm{\epsilon}}^{(1)}}\Bigl[\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1},\hat{\bm{x}}^{(2)}_{t+1},\ldots,\hat{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{t+1}|{\bm{0}})|{\bm{\epsilon}}^{(1)}]\Bigr]

(D.124)

\displaystyle\leq C_{6,T}\sum_{t\in N\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{\epsilon}}^{(1)}}[\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{\epsilon}}|{\bm{\epsilon}}^{(1)}}\Bigl[\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1},\hat{\bm{x}}^{(2)}_{t+1},\ldots,\hat{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{t+1}|{\bm{0}})|{\bm{\epsilon}}^{(1)}]\Bigr],

(D.125)

we get

\sum_{t\in N\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}}^{T}\tilde{c}^{(1)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1}|{\bm{0}})\leq 8C_{5}\log\frac{5C_{5}}{\delta}NC_{6,T}^{N+1}+C_{6,T}^{N+1}\sum_{t\in N\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}}^{T}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\sum_{i=1}^{M^{(n)}}\sigma^{(n)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},i,t}({\bm{y}}^{(n-1)}_{t+n-1},{\bm{x}}^{(n)}_{t+n}).

(D.126)

Thus, noting that
(a+b)^{2}\leq 2a^{2}+2b^{2}
, using the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality and
\tilde{\gamma}_{T}
we have

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{t\in N\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}}^{T}\tilde{c}^{(1)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1}|{\bm{0}})\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left(8C_{5}\log\frac{5C_{5}}{\delta}NC_{6,T}^{N+1}\right)^{2}+2C^{2(N+1)}_{6,T}TM_{\text{sum}}\sum_{n=1}^{N}\sum_{i=1}^{M^{(n)}}\sigma^{(n)2}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},i,t}({\bm{y}}^{(n-1)}_{t+n-1},{\bm{x}}^{(n)}_{t+n})

(D.127)

\displaystyle\leq 2\left(8C_{5}\log\frac{5C_{5}}{\delta}NC_{6,T}^{N+1}\right)^{2}+2C^{2(N+1)}_{6,T}TM_{\text{sum}}\frac{2M_{\text{sum}}\tilde{\gamma}_{T}}{\log(1+\sigma^{-2})}

(D.128)

\displaystyle=C^{2(N+1)}_{6,T}(C_{7}+C_{8}T\tilde{\gamma}_{T}).

(D.129)

This implies that

\displaystyle\sum_{t\in N\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}}^{T}\tilde{c}^{(1)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1}|{\bm{0}})\leq\sqrt{C^{2(N+1)}_{6,T}(C_{7}+C_{8}T\tilde{\gamma}_{T})}.

(D.130)

Furthermore, letting
\tilde{t}=\argmax_{t\in N\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},t\leq T}\tilde{c}^{(1)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1}|{\bm{0}})

we get

\displaystyle K\tilde{c}^{(1)}_{\tilde{t}}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{\tilde{t}+1}|{\bm{0}})

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{t\in N\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0}}^{T}\tilde{c}^{(1)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{t+1}|{\bm{0}})

(D.131)

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{C^{2(N+1)}_{6,T}(C_{7}+C_{8}T\tilde{\gamma}_{T})}.

(D.132)

By dividing both sides by
K
, we obtain

\displaystyle\tilde{c}^{(1)}_{\tilde{t}}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{\tilde{t}+1}|{\bm{0}})

\displaystyle\leq K^{-1}\sqrt{C^{2(N+1)}_{6,T}(C_{7}+C_{8}T\tilde{\gamma}_{T})}

(D.133)

\displaystyle=\frac{N}{T}\sqrt{C^{2(N+1)}_{6,T}(C_{7}+C_{8}T\tilde{\gamma}_{T})}.

(D.134)

Finally, from the definition of the estimated solution and CIs, we get

\displaystyle G({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{G,\ast},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{G,\ast})

\displaystyle\leq\min_{t\in N\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},t\leq T}\mathrm{UCB}^{(G)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{G,\ast},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{G,\ast})

\displaystyle\leq\mathrm{UCB}^{(G)}_{\tilde{t}}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{G,\ast},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{G,\ast}),

\displaystyle G(\hat{\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{G,T},\ldots,\hat{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{G,T})

\displaystyle\geq\max_{t\in N\mathbb{Z}_{\geq 0},t\leq T}\mathrm{LCB}^{(G)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)})

\displaystyle\geq\mathrm{LCB}^{(G)}_{\tilde{t}}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{G,\ast},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{G,\ast}).

Thus, it follows that

\displaystyle G({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{G,\ast},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{G,\ast})-G(\hat{\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{G,T},\ldots,\hat{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{G,T})

\displaystyle\leq 2\beta^{1/2}_{T}\mathbb{E}_{\bm{\epsilon}}[\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)}_{{\bm{\epsilon}},1,\tilde{t}}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{G,\ast},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{G,\ast})]

(D.135)

\displaystyle\leq 2\beta^{1/2}_{T}\tilde{b}^{(1)}_{\tilde{t}}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{G,\ast}|{\bm{0}})

(D.136)

\displaystyle\leq 2{\beta}^{1/2}_{T}\eta^{-1}_{\tilde{t}}\tilde{c}^{(1)}_{\tilde{t}}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{G,\ast}|{\bm{0}})\leq 2{\beta}^{1/2}_{T}\eta^{-1}_{T}\tilde{c}^{(1)}_{\tilde{t}}({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{\tilde{t}+1}|{\bm{0}}).

(D.137)

Hence, by combining this and
eq.

D.134
, we have

\displaystyle G({\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{G,\ast},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{G,\ast})-G(\hat{\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{G,T},\ldots,\hat{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{G,T})

\displaystyle\leq 2{\beta}^{1/2}_{T}\eta^{-1}_{T}\frac{N}{T}\sqrt{C^{2(N+1)}_{6,T}(C_{7}+C_{8}T\tilde{\gamma}_{T})}

(D.138)

\displaystyle\leq C_{6,T}\frac{N}{T}\sqrt{C^{2(N+1)}_{6,T}(C_{7}+C_{8}T\tilde{\gamma}_{T})}

(D.139)

\displaystyle=\frac{N}{T}\sqrt{C^{2(N+2)}_{6,T}(C_{7}+C_{8}T\tilde{\gamma}_{T})}<\xi.

(D.140)

∎

D.4
Optimistic Improvement-based AF for the Final Stage Output

We give an optimistic improvement-based AF for
F
under the noisy setting.
First, we define the sum of the squares of the observation noise
\epsilon_{\text{sum}}
as

\epsilon_{\text{sum}}=\sum_{n=1}^{N}\sum_{m=1}^{M^{(n)}}\epsilon^{(n)2}_{m}.

(D.141)

Note that
\epsilon_{\text{sum}}
is bounded by
M_{\text{sum}}A^{2}
under
D.1
.
Moreover, we assume the following assumption for
\epsilon_{\text{sum}}
.

Assumption D.12
.

Under
D.1
, there exists a positive constant
C
such that

\mathbb{P}(\epsilon_{\text{sum}}<V)>CV
for any
V
with
0<V\leq M_{\text{sum}}A^{2}
.

For example, if
\epsilon_{\text{sum}}
is a discrete random variable with
\mathbb{P}(\epsilon_{\text{sum}}=0)>0
,
then
D.12
holds.
Similarly, if
\epsilon_{\text{sum}}
is a continuous random variable whose probability density function
p_{\epsilon_{\text{sum}}}(x)

satisfies
p_{\epsilon_{\text{sum}}}(x)>K>0
, where
x
is an arbitrary element of some interval
[0,U]
.
Then,
D.12
also holds.
Thus,
D.12
guarantees that
\epsilon_{\text{sum}}
can take values within an arbitrary neighborhood of zero.
Next, we define the variable
C_{9,t}
as

C_{9,t}=2\beta^{1/2}_{t}\eta^{-1}_{t}(2\beta^{1/2}_{t}+2)^{N}C^{N}_{3,t}\eta^{-N}_{t},

(D.142)

where
\eta_{t}=(1+\log t)^{-1}
.
Then, we assume the following assumption.

Assumption D.13
.

For any
T\geq 1
,
C_{9,t}
satisfies that

\sum_{t=T}^{t^{\prime}}C^{-2}_{9,t}\to\infty\quad(\text{as}\ t^{\prime}\to\infty).

(D.143)

Note that
C_{9,t}
is a polynomial function on
\beta_{t}
.
Furthermore, by considering the definition of
\beta_{t}
, the closed form of the mutual information, and
\tilde{\gamma}_{t}
, we can show that the order of
C_{9,t}
is expressed as the polynomial function of
\tilde{\gamma}_{t}
.
Here, under certain conditions, it is known that the order of
\tilde{\gamma}_{t}
for commonly used kernels such as Gaussian kernels and linear kernels is a logarithmic order
Srinivas et al., 2010
.
Then,
D.13
holds if we use such kernels.
Under this setting, we propose an algorithm to the regret
r^{(\text{S})}_{F,T}
.

First, for each
t\geq 1
, we define the estimated solution
\hat{\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{F,t},\dots,\hat{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{F,t}
as follows:

\hat{\bm{x}}^{(1)}_{F,t},\dots,\hat{\bm{x}}^{(N)}_{F,t}=\argmax_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq t^{\prime}\leq t\\
({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)})\in\mathcal{X}\end{subarray}}(\tilde{z}^{(N)}_{{\bm{0}},1,t^{\prime}}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)})-\beta^{1/2}_{t}\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)}_{{\bm{0}},1,t^{\prime}}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)})).

(D.144)

Then, we give the optimistic improvement-based AF.
For any
n\in[N]
, given an observation
\bm{y}^{(n-1)}
of stage
n-1
,
optimistic maximum estimator
\widehat{\mathrm{UCB}}^{(F)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})
w.r.t.
\bm{x}^{(n)}
is defined as:

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathrm{UCB}}^{(F)}_{t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})

(D.145)

\displaystyle=\max_{({\bm{x}}^{(n+1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)})}\Bigl(\tilde{{z}}^{(N)}_{{\bm{0}},1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})+\beta^{1/2}_{t}\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)}_{{\bm{0}},1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})\Bigr),

(D.146)

where the max operator is not needed when
n=N
.
Similarly, pessimistic maximum estimator
\widehat{\mathrm{LCB}}^{(F)}_{t}({\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})
under given an observation
\bm{y}^{(n-1)}
is defined as follows:

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathrm{LCB}}^{(F)}_{t}({\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})

(D.147)

\displaystyle=\max_{({\bm{x}}^{(n)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)})}\Bigl(\tilde{z}^{(N)}_{{\bm{0}},1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})-\beta^{1/2}_{t}\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)}_{{\bm{0}},1,t}({\bm{x}}^{(n)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)}|{\bm{y}}^{(n-1)})\Bigr).

(D.148)

Moreover, pessimistic maximum estimator of
F
is given by:

\hat{Q}_{T}=\max_{({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)})}\Bigl(\tilde{z}^{(N)}_{{\bm{0}},1,T}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)})-\beta^{1/2}_{t}\tilde{\sigma}^{(N)}_{{\bm{0}},1,T}({\bm{x}}^{(1)},\ldots,{\bm{x}}^{(N)})\Bigr).

(D.149)
